57	## Review
58	Run by the reviewer agent: the interface review after Phase 0, a phase review per package at each checkpoint, and the milestone exit. `dt land` already checked each landing's `Writes:`, protected paths and gates; review judges what tools can't.
59	
60	**Interface** (after Phase 0): the code is the contract, no more and no less.
61	1. Types match `contract.md`: fields, types, invariants. Nothing a step's In → Out needs is missing; no helper is frozen.
62	2. Signatures match the step tables, in their owner modules. Each stub names the open task that owns its step; no logic beyond types and validation.
63	3. One sample per case the contract names, valid and realistic, never holding a TC's expected value. Replay doubles replay an interaction the Protocol allows. Each fake passes its contract's cases; anything it does beyond echoing a fixture (filtering, ordering, an error) is a case the real provider must pass too. Contract tests check shape only, never a stub or other state a later task changes.
64	4. `__init__.py` exports exactly the contract; each `Mitigated by` in the plan's dependency graph exists; each split task's tester can write its tests from the frozen interface and its `Read:`.
65	
66	**Each phase**, per package, tests before code:
67	1. **Interfaces followed** (CR-09): no frozen type, signature or contract folder changed outside a Revise. Consumers use the declared types: no dict for a dataclass, no reaching into a provider's internals, no local redefinition. A stub is replaced only by the task it names; no cross-package import outside the contract; spine tests run on samples and doubles, not another step's code.
68	2. **Tests prove something:** each intent test asserts its TC case or acceptance bullet, loading expected values and tolerances from fixtures; none is assertion-free or weakened; Hypothesis runs the `ci` profile. Unit tests are specific, never computing the expected value with the code under test or testing only a mock. **Mutation check** (`.claude/agents/reviewer.md`): per acceptance bullet, the smallest change that should break it; a mutation no test catches is a finding.
69	3. **Correct to the spec:** each acceptance bullet truly met, not special-cased to the sample; the error paths the requirement names; seeding and determinism; numerical edge cases.
70	4. **Simple** (CR-11, CR-12, CR-42): no abstraction with one implementation outside the contract or an AD; no forwarding wrapper, one-method class, or class where a function does; no parameter, flag or config key every caller sets the same; no `try/except` that only re-raises or swallows, no checks the types already guarantee; no loop the library already provides. No near-copies within or across the phase's tasks: one concept is one function in its owner module (tests may repeat for readability). Each finding names the simpler form, with the same tests passing.
71	5. **Pipelines** (CR-10): entry functions read as their step tables (`dt layout`); step owners respected; spine baselines still selectable.
72	6. **The bar** (CR-07): diff this file, `pyproject.toml`, `scripts/` and `.claude/agents/` against the milestone's Base. Any loosening is Critical; new exceptions need an owner and expiry.
73	7. **Hidden and parallel risks:** where a real provider could act differently from its double; fixed output paths, global state, test-order dependence.
74	8. **The rest:** readability, security, performance where a QR sets a number; reused reference or probe code has its Caveats handled and fits the contract; `dt lint` clean, pre-build sections replaced by `dt layout` pointers.
75	
76	**Milestone exit**, over the whole diff, adds: the Scope's Exit holds; every Covers ID has a test; `check.sh full --exit` finds no stub; system tests use real providers; the same sample through a real provider and its double agrees; deferred requirements untouched; pip-audit ran.
77	
78	**Severity:** Critical: a frozen interface changed, a contract test pinning a transient state, a weakened test, an intent test that misses a mutation changing its TC's expected output, a wrong answer, a loosened bar, a security hole, lost data; it stops the build. Important: a consumer bypassing a type, any other surviving mutation, an unneeded abstraction, a duplicate across tasks. Suggestion and Nit: local. Findings go under the checkpoint block in `tasks/plan.md` (`dt finding`), each with a fix task or a reason.
79

2	# One entry point for every quality check. Hooks call it; so do CI and people.

9	#   scripts/check.sh full [--pkg ID]     checkpoints, CI                 (reviewer)

22	# an ImportError or AttributeError.

25	# deptry too); gate and full also run tests/contracts, whole, when it exists. Lint, types, size
26	# and boundaries always cover the whole repo; they take seconds.

56	    rllab) echo "" ;;

79	
80	if [ -n "$pkg" ] && ! pkg_scope "$pkg" >/dev/null; then
81	  echo "unknown package '$pkg' (see pkg_scope in scripts/check.sh)" >&2
82	  exit 64
83	fi
84	
85	rc=0
86	failed=()
87	
88	# A step that is not a Python tool (a script, git, another toolchain).
89	step() {
90	  echo "-> $*" >&2
91	  "$@" || { rc=1; failed+=("$*"); }
92	}
93	
94	# A tool from the project's locked environment, never whatever is first on PATH.
95	run() {
96	  echo "-> $*" >&2
97	  uv run --frozen --quiet "$@" || { rc=1; failed+=("$*"); }
98	}
99	
100	# A measured check that fails only at the milestone exit: it prints its findings, and the reviewer
101	# records them, but a mid-milestone run passes.
102	report() {
103	  echo "-> $* (reported; fails only with --exit)" >&2
104	  uv run --frozen --quiet "$@" || echo "(reported, not failing: $*)" >&2
105	}
106	
107	# pytest exits 5 when it collects nothing. That is fine for gate and full on a project with no
108	# tests yet; collect calls pytest through run() so an empty tests/intent fails.
109	run_tests() {
110	  echo "-> pytest $*" >&2
111	  uv run --frozen --quiet pytest "$@"
112	  local status=$?
113	  [ "$status" -eq 5 ] && { echo "(no tests collected)" >&2; return; }
114	  [ "$status" -eq 0 ] || { rc=1; failed+=("pytest $*"); }
115	}
116	
117	# The commit this task started from: refs/worktree/checks-base, set per worktree by `dt claim` (and
118	# by `dt land` in the main checkout). Without it, HEAD, which misses changes already committed.
119	BASE_REF="refs/worktree/checks-base"
120	base_commit() {
121	  git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" 2>/dev/null || git rev-parse -q --verify HEAD 2>/dev/null
122	}
123	
124	# A boundary contract is frozen once its folder exists at the base (CR-09): each folder under
125	# tests/contracts/ at the base, and any file directly in it. A folder new since the base is the
126	# interface task freezing it, so that task may write it; the next task's base freezes it. A frozen
127	# folder the task's .claude/.task_writes names (from its Writes:, by dt claim) is a re-freeze
128	# interface task's, so it is open to that task; dt land checks the Writes: against the plan.
129	CONTRACTS_GLOB="tests/contracts/**"
130	claimed_contract() {
131	  local line
132	  [ -s .claude/.task_writes ] || return 1
133	  while IFS= read -r line; do
134	    line="${line%%[[:space:]]}"
135	    line="${line%/}"; line="${line%/\*\*}"
136	    [ "$line" = "$1" ] && return 0
137	  done <.claude/.task_writes
138	  return 1
139	}
140	frozen_contracts() {
141	  local base meta path
142	  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
143	  git ls-tree "$base" -- tests/contracts/ 2>/dev/null | while IFS=$'\t' read -r meta path; do
144	    case "$meta" in
145	      *" tree "*) claimed_contract "$path" || echo "$path/**" ;;
146	      *) echo "$path" ;;
147	    esac
148	  done
149	}
150	
151	# The protected list as enforced: PROTECTED, with tests/contracts/** narrowed to the frozen contracts.
152	protected_globs() {
153	  local g
154	  for g in "${PROTECTED[@]}"; do
155	    if [ "$g" = "$CONTRACTS_GLOB" ]; then frozen_contracts; else echo "$g"; fi
156	  done
157	}
158	
159	# Fail if anything matching the given pathspecs changed since the base, untracked files included.
160	unchanged_since_base() {
161	  local label="$1" base new
162	  shift
163	  echo "-> $label unchanged since $(git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "$BASE_REF" || echo HEAD)" >&2
164	  if ! base=$(base_commit); then
165	    echo "no commit to compare against: commit the tester's work first" >&2
166	    rc=1; failed+=("$label: no commit"); return
167	  fi
168	  git --no-pager diff --exit-code --stat "$base" -- "$@" >&2 || { rc=1; failed+=("$label changed"); }
169	  new=$(git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$@")
170	  if [ -n "$new" ]; then
171	    printf 'new untracked files in %s:\n%s\n' "$label" "$new" >&2
172	    rc=1; failed+=("$label changed")
173	  fi
174	}
175	
176	# The tester may change tests/intent only. Docs and plans are the plugin's business, not this check's.
177	only_intent_changed() {
178	  local specs=(tests "${SRC_DIRS[@]}" "${PROTECTED[@]}")
179	  [ -n "$PACKAGES_DIR" ] && specs+=("$PACKAGES_DIR")
180	  unchanged_since_base "files outside tests/intent" "${specs[@]}" ':(exclude)tests/intent'
181	}
182	
183	# The intent test files the tester changed since the base, committed or not.
184	changed_intent_tests() {
185	  local base
186	  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
187	  { git diff --relative --name-only --diff-filter=d "$base" -- 'tests/intent/*.py'
188	    git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- 'tests/intent/*.py'; } | sort -u |
189	    while IFS= read -r f; do case "${f##*/}" in test_*|*_test.py) echo "$f" ;; esac; done
190	}
191	
192	# Test directories for this run: all of them, or the --pkg scope; tests/contracts always whole.
193	test_dirs() {
194	  local scope="" d p
195	  [ -n "$pkg" ] && scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
196	  for d in tests/intent tests/unit; do
197	    if [ -z "$scope" ]; then
198	      [ -d "$d" ] && echo "$d"
199	    else
200	      for p in $scope; do [ -d "$d/$p" ] && echo "$d/$p"; done
201	    fi
202	  done
203	  [ -d tests/contracts ] && echo tests/contracts
204	}
205	
206	# deptry checks what each package imports against what it declares.
207	dependencies() {
208	  local p scope
209	  if [ -z "$PACKAGES_DIR" ]; then
210	    run deptry . --no-ansi
211	    return
212	  fi
213	  if [ -n "$pkg" ]; then
214	    scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
215	  else
216	    scope=$(cd "$PACKAGES_DIR" && for d in */pyproject.toml; do [ -f "$d" ] && dirname "$d"; done)
217	  fi
218	  for p in $scope; do
219	    echo "-> deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p" >&2
220	    (cd "$PACKAGES_DIR/$p" && uv run --frozen --quiet deptry . --no-ansi) || { rc=1; failed+=("deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p"); }
221	  done
222	}
223	
224	static_checks() {
225	  run ruff check --ignore-noqa .   # exceptions go in per-file-ignores, which is protected (CR-01)
226	  run ruff format --check .
227	  run "$TYPE_GATE"
228	  run python scripts/check_size.py "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"
229	  dependencies
230	  run lint-imports --no-logo
231	}
232	
233	case "$mode" in
234	  fast)
235	    # Only Python files, only the files just edited. Fixes what it can in place.
236	    files=()
237	    for f in ${args[@]+"${args[@]}"}; do [[ $f == *.py ]] && files+=("$f"); done
238	    [ ${#files[@]} -eq 0 ] && exit 0
239	    run ruff check --fix --quiet "${files[@]}"
240	    run ruff format --quiet "${files[@]}"
241	    run typos "${files[@]}"
242	    run python scripts/check_size.py "${files[@]}"
243	    ;;
244	  collect)
245	    if [ ! -d tests/intent ]; then
246	      echo "tests/intent does not exist: write the intent tests there" >&2
247	      exit 1
248	    fi
249	    run ruff check --ignore-noqa tests/intent
250	    run ruff format --check tests/intent
251	    run typos tests/intent
252	    run pytest tests/intent --collect-only -q --no-cov
253	    changed=()
254	    while IFS= read -r f; do changed+=("$f"); done < <(changed_intent_tests)
255	    run python scripts/red_check.py ${changed[@]+"${changed[@]}"}
256	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
257	    only_intent_changed
258	    ;;
259	  gate)
260	    static_checks
261	    other_parts
262	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
263	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086  # one directory per word, by construction
264	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && run_tests $dirs --no-cov -q -x
265	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
266	    protected=()
267	    while IFS= read -r g; do protected+=("$g"); done < <(protected_globs)
268	    unchanged_since_base "protected files" "${protected[@]}"
269	    ;;
270	  full)
271	    static_checks
272	    other_parts
273	    if [ "$TYPE_GATE" = mypy ]; then run pyright; else run mypy; fi
274	    # CR-41: the interface task's stubs and fields stay unused until later tasks use them.
275	    if [ ${#exit_check[@]} -gt 0 ]; then run vulture; else report vulture; fi
276	    run pylint --disable=all --enable=duplicate-code --recursive=y --score=n --fail-under=10 "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"   # CR-42
277	    run typos
278	    run pip-audit --skip-editable   # the external opinion: a vulnerability database (CR-26)
279	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh ${exit_check[@]+"${exit_check[@]}"}
280	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
281	    # Coverage threshold comes from [tool.coverage.report] fail_under (CR-40).
282	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
283	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && HYPOTHESIS_PROFILE=ci run_tests $dirs --cov -q
284	    ;;
285	  protected)
286	    protected_globs
287	    ;;
288	  *)
289	    echo "unknown mode: $mode (fast | collect | gate | full | protected)" >&2
290	    exit 64
291	    ;;
292	esac
293	[ $rc -ne 0 ] && printf 'FAILED: %s\n' "${failed[@]}" >&2
294	exit $rc
295

3	**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-19 · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)

5	## core
6	
7	### core.Env: type
8	```python
9	class Env(Protocol):
10	    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
11	    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
12	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
13	    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult: ...
14	    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray: ...
15	```
16	- **Invariants:** teams, each batch-first (AD-09, AD-13): observations map each team name to float32 `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` from its `TeamSpec`, and `actions` maps each team to `(n_agents,)` int64 when discrete or `(n_agents, dim)` float32 when continuous. Single-team environments have one team, `"agents"` (`core.SOLO`). Entries for agents already done are ignored. `info` always holds `steps`, `score:<team>` for each team, and `score` for the first team. `frame()` is an int8 grid of cell codes (0 empty, 1 head, 2 body, 3 food or goal) when `board == "grid"`, and a `(K, 4)` float32 array of circles `(x, y, radius, kind)` when `board == "tank"`, with kinds 0 ball, 1 fish, 2 caught fish, 3 predator, 4 prey, 5 caught prey, 6 pellet (AD-12). The same seed and actions give the same results.
17	- **Errors:** `RuntimeError` if `step` is called after the game is over. `ValueError` for a missing team, an action of the wrong shape, outside the discrete range, or non-finite.
18	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Env/ · **Cases:** a recorded 20-step Snake game ending in a wall hit; a recorded 9-step corridor game reaching the goal; a recorded 30-step 8-fish tank game with 2 catches; a recorded 40-step predator-prey game with 1 catch and 2 pellets eaten
19	- **Used by:** envs (provider), train, evaluate, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06
20	
21	### core.TeamSpec: type
22	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TeamSpec: n_agents: int; obs_shape: tuple[int, ...]; actions: ActionSpace`
23	- **Invariants:** `n_agents ≥ 1`; `obs_shape` is per agent. A team's agents all share one view and one action space, so one `core.Agent` can act for the whole team.
24	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines, reference, train, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** Snake's one compact-view agent; the tank's 8 full-view fish; predator-prey's predator (1 × 19) and prey (6 × 18) teams
25	- **Used by:** envs, agents, baselines, reference, train, lab; PL-01, PL-03
26	
27	### core.SOLO: Single-team name
28	`SOLO: str = "agents"`
29	- **Invariants:** the one team name every single-team environment uses (Snake, the corridor, the fish tank).
30	- **Boundary:** envs, train, evaluate, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.SOLO/ · **Cases:** the constant
31	- **Used by:** envs, train, evaluate, lab; PL-01, PL-03
32	
33	### core.ActionSpace: type
34	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ActionSpace: kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]; n: int = 0; dim: int = 0`
35	- **Invariants:** discrete: `n ≥ 2` and `dim == 0`. Continuous: `dim ≥ 1`, `n == 0`, and the environment clips each action itself (FISH-02).
36	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** Snake's 3 discrete actions; the tank's 2-D continuous swim vector
37	- **Used by:** envs, agents, baselines, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-03
38	
39	### core.StepResult: type
40	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StepResult: obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]; reward: dict[str, np.ndarray]; done: dict[str, np.ndarray]; terminated: bool; truncated: bool; info: dict[str, float]`
41	- **Invariants:** each map has one entry per team. `reward[team]` is `(n_agents,)` float32 and `done[team]` `(n_agents,)` bool, true while an agent is out (a collision, the goal, a catch, or a caught prey waiting to reappear). `terminated` means the game itself ended (every single-team agent done); `truncated` means a step cap or starvation ended the game first. At most one of the two is true. `info` holds `steps`, `score:<team>` per team and `score`.
42	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.StepResult/ · **Cases:** an ordinary step; eating food; a wall collision; starvation truncation; a tank step with one fish caught; the tank's 1,000-step truncation; a predator-prey step with a catch
43	- **Used by:** envs, train, evaluate, reference; PL-01, PL-03
44	
45	### core.Transition: type
46	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: obs: np.ndarray; actions: np.ndarray; reward: np.ndarray; next_obs: np.ndarray; terminated: np.ndarray; active: np.ndarray; truncated: bool`
47	- **Invariants:** one `Transition` per learning team per step, every array batch-first over that team's agents. `active` marks agents that acted this step. `terminated` is true per agent only for that agent's real end (a collision, the corridor goal or a catch), never for truncation, so truncated steps still bootstrap.
48	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Transition/ · **Cases:** a food step; a collision step; a truncated step; a tank step with 8 active fish of which one is caught
49	- **Used by:** train, agents, baselines; PL-01
50	
51	### core.Agent: type
52	```python
53	class Agent(Protocol):
54	    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray: ...
55	    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...
56	    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
57	    def load(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
58	```
59	- **Invariants:** one agent acts for one team: `act` takes that team's `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` and returns one action per agent, shaped by the team's `ActionSpace`. `explore=False` never samples or takes a random action (AGENT-07): value learners take the best-valued action and policy learners the policy's mean. Players that don't learn make `learn` a no-op. `save` then `load` gives the same `act` outputs.
60	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Agent/ · **Cases:** a replay double recording 5 act/learn calls of a tabular agent
61	- **Used by:** agents, baselines, reference (providers); train, evaluate, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05
62	
63	### core.RunSettings: type
64	```python
65	@dataclass(frozen=True)
66	class RunSettings:
67	    name: str; env: Literal["snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"]; view: Literal["compact", "grid", "index", "full", "limited"]
68	    agent: Literal["tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference"]; n_agents: int
69	    opponents: Mapping[str, Literal["learn", "scripted", "random"]]; self_play: Literal["none", "latest", "pool"]
70	    pool_latest: float; generations: int
71	    ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target", "no_explore"]
72	    rewards: Rewards; exploration: Exploration; params: AgentParams
73	    steps: int; seed: int; eval_every: int; eval_games: int
74	```
75	- **Invariants:** `seed` in `[0, 1_000_000)`. `steps` > 0. `view` belongs to `env` (Snake `compact`/`grid`, corridor `index`, tank `full`/`limited`). `n_agents` is 1 except for the tank (1–16, FISH-07). `reinforce` and `ppo` only with the tank; `tabular` and `dqn` only with Snake or the corridor. `reference` on the tank needs `n_agents == 1` and `full`. For `predprey`, `view` is `limited`, `agent` is `ppo` or `reference`, and `opponents` names both teams (`predator`, `prey`): `learn` teams train with `agent`; `self_play` is `latest` or `pool` only when both learn (rung 2, rung 3), `none` otherwise; `generations` is 10 when any team learns, else 0; `pool_latest` defaults to 0.5 (SELF-03). Single-team runs have `opponents == {}`. `ablation` matches `agent` (`no_memory` and `no_target` with `dqn`, `no_explore` with `tabular`).
76	- **Boundary:** lab, train, runs, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.RunSettings/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact ladder settings; a corridor tabular settings; a no_target ablation; the rung-3 tank settings (ppo, 8 fish, full view); the tank reference settings; predator-prey rung 1 (prey learn, scripted predator); rung 3 (both learn, pool)
77	- **Used by:** lab, train, runs, reference, agents; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-06
78	
79	### core.Rewards: type
80	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Rewards: food: float = 1.0; death: float = -1.0; step: float = 0.0`
81	- **Invariants:** for the corridor, `food` is the goal reward. For the tank, `step` is the reward per surviving fish-step and `death` the reward on a catch; tank settings default to `step = 1.0`, `death = 0.0` (FISH-05).
82	- **Boundary:** envs · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Rewards/ · **Cases:** defaults; food set to +10; the tank's survival rewards
83	- **Used by:** envs, lab; PL-01, PL-02
84	
85	### core.Exploration: type
86	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Exploration: start: float = 1.0; end: float = 0.05; decay_steps: int = 50_000`
87	- **Invariants:** `0 ≤ end ≤ start ≤ 1`. The rate falls linearly to `end` at `decay_steps`, then stays there (AGENT-02).
88	- **Boundary:** agents, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Exploration/ · **Cases:** defaults; no decay (`start == end`)
89	- **Used by:** agents, reference; PL-01, PL-02
90	
91	### core.AgentParams: type
92	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class AgentParams: lr: float; gamma: float; batch_size: int = 64; memory_size: int = 50_000; target_every: int = 1_000; learn_start: int = 1_000; hidden: int = 128; net: Literal["mlp", "cnn"] = "mlp"; rollout_steps: int = 2048; epochs: int = 10; clip: float = 0.2; gae_lambda: float = 0.95; games_per_update: int = 10`
93	- **Invariants:** `0 < gamma ≤ 1`. The table-based agent reads only `lr` (its step size) and `gamma`. Policy-gradient agents read `lr`, `gamma`, `hidden` and `batch_size`; PPO also `rollout_steps`, `epochs`, `clip`, `gae_lambda`; REINFORCE also `games_per_update` (AD-11).
94	- **Boundary:** agents, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.AgentParams/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact defaults; tabular `lr=0.1, gamma=0.9`; the ppo defaults (`lr=3e-4, gamma=0.99, hidden=64`)
95	- **Used by:** agents, reference; PL-01, PL-02
96	
97	### core.load_settings: Read run settings
98	`def load_settings(path: Path) -> RunSettings`
99	- **Invariants:** unspecified fields take the dataclass defaults. The result is fully resolved, so no other input is needed (RUN-01).
100	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the key for an unknown environment, view, agent, ablation, or an out-of-range value. `FileNotFoundError` if the file is missing.
101	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.load_settings/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact ladder file; a file naming an unknown agent
102	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-02
103	
104	## envs
105	
106	### envs.Snake: Snake game
107	```python
108	class Snake(Env):
109	    def __init__(self, *, view: Literal["compact", "grid"], rewards: Rewards = Rewards(), size: int = 10) -> None: ...
110	    def reset_to(self, body: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], heading: Literal["up", "right", "down", "left"], food: tuple[int, int]) -> tuple[np.ndarray, dict[str, int]]: ...
111	```
112	- **Invariants:** SNAKE-01–SNAKE-09. `reset_to` starts a game from a given board (cells are `(row, col)`, row 0 at the top, body head first), with score 0 and the step count at 0; later food is seeded as after `reset`. One team, `SOLO`, of 1 agent; `board == "grid"`. Actions: discrete 3, 0 straight, 1 turn left, 2 turn right. The compact observation has per-agent shape `(11,)`, laid out as `COMPACT_FIELDS`. The grid observation has per-agent shape `(3, size, size)`: head, body and food channels. Filling the board ends the game with `terminated=True` and no death reward.
113	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` if the body is not contiguous, leaves the grid, or the food is on the snake
114	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.Snake/ · **Cases:** a recorded compact-view game ending at a wall; a recorded grid-view game eating twice then hitting itself
115	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines; PL-01, PL-03
116	
117	### envs.Corridor: 1-D corridor
118	`class Corridor(Env): def __init__(self, *, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50, rewards: Rewards = Rewards())`
119	- **Invariants:** CORE-03. One team, `SOLO`, of 1 agent; `board == "grid"`. Actions: discrete 2, 0 left, 1 right. The observation is a one-hot position of per-agent shape `(length,)`. `frame()` is `1 × length`.
120	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
121	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.Corridor/ · **Cases:** a recorded 9-step game reaching the goal; a 50-step truncated game
122	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
123	
124	### envs.COMPACT_FIELDS: Compact view layout
125	`COMPACT_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("danger_straight", "danger_left", "danger_right", "head_up", "head_right", "head_down", "head_left", "food_left", "food_right", "food_up", "food_down")`
126	- **Invariants:** index `i` of a compact observation is field `i`. Every value is 0.0 or 1.0.
127	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.COMPACT_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 11-name tuple
128	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-03
129	
130	### envs.FishTank: Fish tank
131	```python
132	class FishTank(Env):
133	    def __init__(self, *, view: Literal["full", "limited"], n_fish: int = 8, rewards: Rewards = Rewards(food=0.0, death=0.0, step=1.0)) -> None: ...
134	    def reset_to(self, fish: Sequence[tuple[float, float]], ball: tuple[float, float], ball_velocity: tuple[float, float]) -> tuple[np.ndarray, dict[str, float]]: ...
135	```
136	- **Invariants:** FISH-01–FISH-10 with the spec's numbers as module constants. One team, `SOLO`, of `n_fish` agents; actions are continuous with `dim == 2`; `board == "tank"`. The full view has per-fish shape `(8,)` laid out as `TANK_FULL_FIELDS`, and the limited view `(9,)` as `TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS`. `info["score"]` is the mean survival time per fish so far. `reset_to` starts from given positions, at step 0.
137	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` for a position outside the tank or a fish within 0.11 of the ball
138	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.FishTank/ · **Cases:** a recorded 30-step 8-fish full-view game with 2 catches; a recorded single-fish limited-view game reaching the 1,000-step cap
139	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines; PL-01, PL-03
140	
141	### envs.TANK_FULL_FIELDS: Tank full-view layout
142	`TANK_FULL_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "ball_dx", "ball_dy", "ball_vx", "ball_vy")`
143	- **Invariants:** index `i` of a full-view observation is field `i`. `ball_dx`, `ball_dy` are the ball's centre minus the fish's.
144	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.TANK_FULL_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 8-name tuple
145	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-03
146	
147	### envs.TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS: Tank limited-view layout
148	`TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "ball_visible", "ball_dx", "ball_dy", "ball_vx", "ball_vy")`
149	- **Invariants:** the five ball fields are 0 unless the ball's centre is within 0.5 of the fish (FISH-10).
150	- **Boundary:** agents · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 9-name tuple
151	- **Used by:** agents; PL-01
152	
153	### envs.PredatorPrey: Predator-prey world
154	```python
155	class PredatorPrey(Env):
156	    def __init__(self, *, rewards: Rewards = Rewards(food=1.0, death=-5.0, step=0.0)) -> None: ...
157	    def reset_to(self, predator: tuple[float, float], prey: Sequence[tuple[float, float]], pellets: Sequence[tuple[float, float]]) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
158	```
159	- **Invariants:** HUNT-01–HUNT-09, with the spec's numbers as module constants and the tank's vectorised physics (AD-16). Teams `"predator"` (1 agent, view `(19,)` as `PREDATOR_FIELDS`) and `"prey"` (6 agents, view `(18,)` as `PREY_FIELDS`), both continuous with `dim == 2`; `board == "tank"`. `rewards.food` is the prey's pellet reward and `rewards.death` its catch penalty; the predator earns +1 per catch. `info` holds `score:predator` (catches so far) and `score:prey` (mean reward per prey so far). The game only truncates, at step 1,000.
160	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` for a position outside the tank, a count other than 6 prey or 8 pellets, or a prey within 0.4 of the predator
161	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.PredatorPrey/ · **Cases:** a recorded 40-step game with 1 catch and 2 pellets eaten; a recorded 60-step game where a caught prey reappears
162	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines, selfplay; PL-01, PL-03, PL-06
163	
164	### envs.PREY_FIELDS: Prey view layout
165	`PREY_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "pred_visible", "pred_dx", "pred_dy", "pred_vx", "pred_vy", "p1_visible", "p1_dx", "p1_dy", "p2_visible", "p2_dx", "p2_dy", "p3_visible", "p3_dx", "p3_dy")`
166	- **Invariants:** HUNT-05: pellets ordered nearest first; out-of-range slots are 0 with their flag 0.
167	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.PREY_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 18-name tuple
168	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-01, PL-03
169	
170	### envs.PREDATOR_FIELDS: Predator view layout
171	`PREDATOR_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "q1_visible", "q1_dx", "q1_dy", "q1_vx", "q1_vy", "q2_visible", "q2_dx", "q2_dy", "q2_vx", "q2_vy", "q3_visible", "q3_dx", "q3_dy", "q3_vx", "q3_vy")`
172	- **Invariants:** HUNT-06: swimming prey ordered nearest first; out-of-range slots are 0 with their flag 0.
173	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.PREDATOR_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 19-name tuple
174	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-01, PL-03

176	## baselines
177	
178	### baselines.RandomPlayer: Random baseline
179	`class RandomPlayer(Agent): def __init__(self, n_actions: int, *, seed: int)`
180	- **Invariants:** uniform over actions whatever `explore` says (BASE-01). `learn` is a no-op.
181	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomPlayer/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 10 seeded `act` calls
182	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
183	
184	### baselines.ScriptedPlayer: Scripted baseline
185	`class ScriptedPlayer(Agent): def __init__(self) -> None`
186	- **Invariants:** BASE-02 on the compact view, deterministic. Ties go straight, then left, then right.
187	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the observation is not shape `(11,)`
188	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPlayer/ · **Cases:** food ahead and safe; food left but danger left
189	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
190	
191	### baselines.RandomSwimmer: Random tank baseline
192	`class RandomSwimmer(Agent): def __init__(self, n_agents: int, *, seed: int)`
193	- **Invariants:** each agent's swim vector is drawn uniformly from the unit disc, whatever `explore` says: the random fish (BASE-04) and the random predator and prey (BASE-08). `learn` is a no-op.
194	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomSwimmer/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 10 seeded `act` calls for 8 fish
195	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
196	
197	### baselines.ScriptedFish: Scripted tank baseline
198	`class ScriptedFish(Agent): def __init__(self) -> None`
199	- **Invariants:** BASE-05 on the full view: each fish swims at length 1 directly away from the ball's centre; with the ball on its centre, toward the tank's centre, or along `(1, 0)` at the centre. Deterministic.
200	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the observation is not `(n, 8)`
201	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedFish/ · **Cases:** ball at the origin, fish at (0.3, 0.4); ball on the fish's centre
202	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
203	
204	### baselines.ScriptedPredator: Scripted predator
205	`class ScriptedPredator(Agent): def __init__(self) -> None`
206	- **Invariants:** BASE-06 on `PREDATOR_FIELDS`: a length-1 vector toward the nearest visible prey, otherwise toward the tank's centre (or `(1, 0)` at the centre). Deterministic.
207	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the observation is not `(1, 19)`
208	- **Boundary:** lab, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPredator/ · **Cases:** a prey at relative (0.3, 0.4); no prey visible at (0.5, 0)
209	- **Used by:** lab, selfplay; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04
210	
211	### baselines.ScriptedPrey: Scripted prey
212	`class ScriptedPrey(Agent): def __init__(self) -> None`
213	- **Invariants:** BASE-07 on `PREY_FIELDS`, per prey: flee at length 1 when the predator is visible and within 0.3, else head at length 1 for the nearest visible pellet, else `(0, 0)`. Deterministic.
214	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the observation is not `(n, 18)`
215	- **Boundary:** lab, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPrey/ · **Cases:** predator at relative (0.12, 0.16); predator out of range with a pellet at (0.3, 0)
216	- **Used by:** lab, selfplay; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04
217	
218	## agents
219	
220	### agents.TabularAgent: Table-based value learner
221	`class TabularAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, n_actions: int, params: AgentParams, exploration: Exploration, *, seed: int, explore_enabled: bool = True)`
222	- **Invariants:** single-agent (batches of 1). One value per (observation, action), keyed on the observation's bytes and starting at 0. Each `learn` moves the value toward `reward + gamma · max next value`, with no bootstrap when `terminated` (AGENT-01). `explore_enabled=False` is the `no_explore` ablation (ABL-03). The checkpoint is NPZ.
223	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.TabularAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 5 act/learn calls on the corridor
224	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
225	
226	### agents.DQNAgent: Neural value learner
227	`class DQNAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, team: TeamSpec, params: AgentParams, exploration: Exploration, *, seed: int, ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target"] = "none")`
228	- **Invariants:** single-agent (batches of 1). The network's input is `team.obs_shape` and its outputs `team.actions.n`. AGENT-03–AGENT-05 and AD-05. With `ablation="no_memory"`, each update uses only the latest transition (ABL-01). With `ablation="no_target"`, targets come from the online network (ABL-02). `ablation` takes `RunSettings.ablation`'s DQN values. No updates happen before `learn_start` transitions. The checkpoint is a PyTorch state dict.
229	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `team.n_agents != 1`, `team.actions.kind != "discrete"`, or `net="cnn"` and `team.obs_shape` is not 3-D
230	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.DQNAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 5 act/learn calls on the compact view after `learn_start`
231	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
232	
233	### agents.ReinforceAgent: Plain policy-gradient learner
234	`class ReinforceAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, obs_shape: tuple[int, ...], actions: ActionSpace, params: AgentParams, *, seed: int)`
235	- **Invariants:** PG-01, PG-02, AD-11. Collects whole games, and after `games_per_update` of them takes one gradient step on the return-to-go, normalised within the batch. `act(explore=True)` samples the Gaussian; `explore=False` returns its mean. The checkpoint is a PyTorch state dict.
236	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless `actions.kind == "continuous"`
237	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.ReinforceAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of one 3-game update on one fish
238	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
239	
240	### agents.PPOAgent: Clipped policy-gradient learner
241	`class PPOAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, obs_shape: tuple[int, ...], actions: ActionSpace, params: AgentParams, *, seed: int)`
242	- **Invariants:** PG-01, PG-03, PG-04, AD-11. One shared policy and value network acts for every active agent. After `rollout_steps` agent-steps it computes advantages (GAE with `gae_lambda`), then runs `epochs` passes of minibatches of `batch_size` on the clipped objective. Inactive agents' steps never enter a batch. The checkpoint is a PyTorch state dict.
243	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless `actions.kind == "continuous"`
244	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.PPOAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of one 64-step rollout and update on 8 fish
245	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
246	
247	### agents.returns_to_go: Discounted returns-to-go
248	`def returns_to_go(rewards: np.ndarray, gamma: float) -> np.ndarray`
249	- **Invariants:** `G_t = r_t + gamma · G_{t+1}` within one game, with `G` after the last step 0; output has the input's length, float64 (PG-02).
250	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless `0 < gamma ≤ 1` and `rewards` is 1-D
251	- **Boundary:** agents · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.returns_to_go/ · **Cases:** a 3-step game
252	- **Used by:** agents (ReinforceAgent); PL-01
253	
254	### agents.gae: Generalised advantage estimates
255	`def gae(rewards: np.ndarray, values: np.ndarray, terminated: np.ndarray, gamma: float, lam: float) -> np.ndarray`
256	- **Invariants:** `δ_t = r_t + gamma · V(s_{t+1}) · (1 − terminated_t) − V(s_t)`, `A_t = δ_t + gamma · lam · (1 − terminated_t) · A_{t+1}`; `values` has one more entry than `rewards` (the bootstrap value after the last step); output float64 of the rewards' length (PG-03).
257	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the lengths don't match or `lam` is outside `[0, 1]`
258	- **Boundary:** agents · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.gae/ · **Cases:** a 2-step rollout ending in a catch; a 3-step truncated rollout
259	- **Used by:** agents (PPOAgent); PL-01
260	
261	### agents.clipped_objective: Clipped surrogate objective
262	`def clipped_objective(ratio: np.ndarray, advantage: np.ndarray, clip: float) -> np.ndarray`
263	- **Invariants:** elementwise `min(ratio · advantage, clip(ratio, 1 − clip, 1 + clip) · advantage)`, the quantity PPO maximises (PG-03); works on NumPy arrays, and the agent applies the same formula to tensors.
264	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless `0 < clip < 1` and the shapes match
265	- **Boundary:** agents · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.clipped_objective/ · **Cases:** four (ratio, advantage) pairs straddling the clip
266	- **Used by:** agents (PPOAgent); PL-01
267	
268	## runs
269	
270	### runs.CurvePoint: type
271	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurvePoint: step: int; games: int; mean_score: float; elapsed_s: float`
272	- **Invariants:** `step` strictly increases within a run. `mean_score` comes from a greedy evaluation of `eval_games` games.
273	- **Boundary:** train, reference, viz · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.CurvePoint/ · **Cases:** the first point at step 0; a mid-run point
274	- **Used by:** train, reference, viz; PL-01, PL-02, PL-04
275	
276	### runs.GameRecording: type
277	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class GameRecording: seed: int; board: Literal["grid", "tank"]; frames: np.ndarray; rewards: np.ndarray; scores: dict[str, np.ndarray]`
278	- **Invariants:** `frames` holds the start frame plus one per step: T×H×W int8 for `grid`, T×K×4 float32 circles for `tank` (AD-12). `rewards` is the per-step total over all agents, length T−1; `scores` maps each team to its score at every frame, length T.
279	- **Boundary:** evaluate, viz · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.GameRecording/ · **Cases:** a 12-step Snake game ending in a self-collision; a 9-step corridor game; a 30-step 8-fish tank game with 2 catches; a 40-step predator-prey game with 1 catch
280	- **Used by:** evaluate, viz, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-05
281	
282	### runs.RunRecord: type
283	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: path: Path; settings: RunSettings; curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]; status: Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]; checkpoints: dict[str, Path]; generations: dict[str, tuple[Path, ...]]; games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]`
284	- **Invariants:** `settings` round-trips through `settings.json` unchanged. `checkpoints` holds one final checkpoint per learning team (single-team runs: `SOLO`), present when `status` is `complete`. `generations` holds each learning team's numbered generations in order (10 when `settings.generations == 10`, else empty) (RUN-02, SELF-04).
285	- **Boundary:** train, reference, evaluate, viz, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a complete tabular corridor run; an incomplete Snake run with 3 curve points; a complete predator-prey rung-3 run with 10 generations per team
286	- **Used by:** train, reference, evaluate, viz, lab, selfplay; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06
287	
288	### runs.RunWriter: Run folder writer
289	```python
290	class RunWriter:
291	    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None: ...
292	    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = SOLO) -> None: ...
293	    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path: ...
294	    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None: ...
295	    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord: ...
296	```
297	- **Invariants:** each `add_point` is flushed to `curve.csv` at once, so an interrupt keeps the curve. Generations are written as `generations/<team>/<index>` and returned, so a pool can load them during the run. The folder layout follows AD-06.
298	- **Boundary:** train, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunWriter/ · **Cases:** a replay double recording a 3-point run that finishes complete
299	- **Used by:** train, reference; PL-01, PL-02
300	
301	### runs.create_run: Start a run folder
302	`def create_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunWriter`
303	- **Invariants:** writes `settings.json` and `status=running` before returning. The folder name is unique.
304	- **Boundary:** train, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.create_run/ · **Cases:** a fresh corridor run folder
305	- **Used by:** train, reference; PL-01, PL-02
306	
307	### runs.open_run: Read a run folder
308	`def open_run(path: Path) -> RunRecord`
309	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run if the folder or `settings.json` is missing. `ValueError` naming the file if a recording is unreadable.
310	- **Boundary:** evaluate, viz, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.open_run/ · **Cases:** a complete corridor run; a folder missing `settings.json`
311	- **Used by:** evaluate, viz, lab; PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06
312	
313	## evaluate
314	
315	### evaluate.ScoreSet: type
316	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ScoreSet: player: str; train_seed: int; scores: np.ndarray; games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]`
317	- **Invariants:** `scores` has one float score per evaluation game (food eaten, or mean survival steps per fish). `games` holds the first `record` games played.
318	- **Boundary:** compare, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.ScoreSet/ · **Cases:** 200 scripted-player scores for seed 0; 200 random-player scores for seed 0; 200 scripted-fish scores for seed 0
319	- **Used by:** compare, train, lab; PL-03, PL-04
320	
321	### evaluate.eval_seeds: Held-out evaluation seeds
322	`def eval_seeds(train_seed: int, n: int) -> list[int]`
323	- **Invariants:** returns `1_000_000 + 1000·train_seed + i` for `i < n`, never overlapping any training seed (DATA-01).
324	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `n` > 1000 or `train_seed` ≥ 1,000,000
325	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.eval_seeds/ · **Cases:** seeds for train seed 0, n=200
326	- **Used by:** evaluate, train; PL-01, PL-03
327	
328	### evaluate.EvalSpec: type
329	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalSpec: player: str; train_seed: int; team: str = SOLO; n_games: int = 200; record: int = 0`
330	- **Invariants:** what one evaluation scores: `player` names the ScoreSet, `team` is the team scored, `n_games` games on `eval_seeds(train_seed, n_games)`, the first `record` of them recorded. `0 ≤ record ≤ n_games`.
331	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `n_games` < 1 or `record` is outside `0..n_games`
332	- **Boundary:** train, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.EvalSpec/ · **Cases:** the scripted player on seed 0 with the defaults; the predator team on seed 3, 50 games, 5 recorded
333	- **Used by:** evaluate, train, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05
334	
335	### evaluate.evaluate: Greedy evaluation
336	`def evaluate(make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], spec: EvalSpec) -> ScoreSet`
337	- **Invariants:** `agents` has one agent per team of the environment; the scores are `spec.team`'s `score:<team>`, under the ScoreSet's `player` `spec.player` and `train_seed` `spec.train_seed`. Plays `spec.n_games` games on `eval_seeds(spec.train_seed, spec.n_games)` with `explore=False`. It never calls `learn`. It records the first `spec.record` games (REPLAY-03). 200 games of any environment take ≤ 60 s on the laptop (QR-04).
338	- **Boundary:** train, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.evaluate/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the scripted-player ScoreSet for seed 0
339	- **Used by:** train, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05
340	
341	## train
342	
343	### train.train: Training loop
344	`def train(make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord`
345	- **Invariants:** `agents` has one agent per team; the teams `settings.opponents` marks `learn` (or `SOLO` in a single-team run) are learners. Steps the environment for `settings.steps` steps, building one batch-first `Transition` per learning team and calling that team's `learn` after every step (CORE-07). With `settings.generations`, it saves each learner's generation every tenth of the run (SELF-04); with `self_play == "pool"`, each game's opponent for every learner comes from its `selfplay.OpponentPool` (SELF-03, AD-14). Curve points evaluate the first learning team against the run's fixed opposition. It evaluates every `eval_every` steps (including step 0) into a `CurvePoint`. It prints a progress line at least every 30 s (RUN-04). It saves each learner's checkpoint and 5 recorded evaluation games at the end. The same settings give the same curve (RUN-03). It knows nothing environment-specific (CORE-02).
346	- **Errors:** on `KeyboardInterrupt`, finishes the run `incomplete`, then re-raises
347	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.train/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a complete 3-point corridor RunRecord
348	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01
349	
350	## reference
351	
352	### reference.train_reference: Library agent training
353	`def train_reference(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord`
354	- **Invariants:** `settings.agent == "reference"`. Trains Stable-Baselines3 DQN on Snake (AD-04), or PPO on the single-fish tank or on the predator-prey predator against scripted prey (AD-10, AD-15), through the AD-03 adapter with `device="cpu"` and the matching one-to-one settings mapping. Curve points use `evaluate.evaluate` at the same `eval_every`. The checkpoint loads into `ReferencePlayer`.
355	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless the settings are Snake with the compact view, the tank with one fish and the full view, or predator-prey with `opponents == {"predator": "learn", "prey": "scripted"}`
356	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/reference.train_reference/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a complete 3-point reference RunRecord
357	- **Used by:** lab; PL-02
358	
359	### reference.ReferencePlayer: Trained library agent as an Agent
360	`class ReferencePlayer(Agent): def __init__(self, checkpoint: Path)`
361	- **Invariants:** `act` returns the library model's deterministic action (greedy for DQN, the mean for PPO), batch-first with one agent. `learn` is a no-op.
362	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/reference.ReferencePlayer/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 5 greedy `act` calls
363	- **Used by:** lab; PL-03, PL-04
364	
365	## selfplay
366	
367	### selfplay.OpponentPool: type
368	```python
369	class OpponentPool:
370	    def __init__(self, team: str, latest: Agent, load: Callable[[Path], Agent], *, latest_share: float, seed: int) -> None: ...
371	    def add(self, generation: Path) -> None: ...
372	    def sample(self) -> Agent: ...
373	```
374	- **Invariants:** SELF-03: `sample` returns `latest` with probability `latest_share`, otherwise a uniformly chosen added generation loaded through `load`; before any `add`, always `latest`. Seeded and deterministic. Loaded generations are cached and never learn.
375	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.OpponentPool/ · **Cases:** a replay double sampling 20 opponents from a pool of 3 generations
376	- **Used by:** train; PL-01
377	
378	### selfplay.CrossPlay: type
379	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CrossPlay: run: Path; predator: np.ndarray; prey: np.ndarray; games: int`
380	- **Invariants:** `predator[i, j]` and `prey[i, j]` are the mean scores of predator generation `i` against prey generation `j` over `games` held-out games; both are 10 × 10 for a co-learning run (SELF-05).
381	- **Boundary:** viz, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.CrossPlay/ · **Cases:** a 3 × 3 table from a short run; the 10 × 10 shape
382	- **Used by:** viz, lab; PL-06
383	
384	### selfplay.Ratings: type
385	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Ratings: predator: np.ndarray; prey: np.ndarray`
386	- **Invariants:** SELF-06: `predator[i]` is the mean of row `i` of `CrossPlay.predator`, `prey[j]` the mean of column `j` of `CrossPlay.prey`.
387	- **Boundary:** viz, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.Ratings/ · **Cases:** ratings of the 3 × 3 sample
388	- **Used by:** viz, lab; PL-06
389	
390	### selfplay.cross_play: Cross-play table
391	`def cross_play(run: RunRecord, make_env: Callable[[], Env], load: Callable[[str, Path], Agent], *, games: int = 50) -> CrossPlay`
392	- **Invariants:** plays every predator generation against every prey generation of `run` with `explore=False` on `eval_seeds`, never calling `learn` (SELF-05).
393	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the run and the generation if one is missing
394	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.cross_play/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the 3 × 3 CrossPlay
395	- **Used by:** lab; PL-06
396	
397	### selfplay.ratings: Generation ratings
398	`def ratings(table: CrossPlay) -> Ratings`
399	- **Invariants:** SELF-06; pure arithmetic over the table.
400	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.ratings/ · **Cases:** the 3 × 3 sample
401	- **Used by:** lab; PL-06
402	
403	## compare
404	
405	### compare.ClaimSpec: type
406	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ClaimSpec: name: str; a: str; b: str; direction: Literal["greater", "not_worse"]; stretch: bool = False; min_ratio: float | None = None`
407	- **Invariants:** `a` and `b` are player names present in the score input. `min_ratio` (REF-02's 0.9) checks `mean(a) ≥ min_ratio · mean(b)` on top of the test.
408	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.ClaimSpec/ · **Cases:** "dqn-compact greater than random"; "dqn-compact not worse than reference with min_ratio 0.9"; the stretch claim for dqn-grid
409	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
410	
411	### compare.Claim: type
412	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Claim: spec: ClaimSpec; mean_a: float; mean_b: float; p_value: float; p_corrected: float; passed: bool; method: str`
413	- **Invariants:** `method` names what the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill chose (statistic, null distribution, correction). For `greater`, `passed` means significant at 0.05 after correction. For `not_worse`, it means "a worse than b" is not significant, and `min_ratio` holds when set.
414	- **Boundary:** lab, viz · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Claim/ · **Cases:** a passed "greater" claim; a failed "not_worse" claim; a stretch claim that failed
415	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
416	
417	### compare.run_claims: Tested claims and report
418	`def run_claims(scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]], specs: Sequence[ClaimSpec], *, alpha: float = 0.05, charts: Sequence[Chart] = (), out: Path) -> list[Claim]`
419	- **Invariants:** each player's scores are pooled across its 5 training seeds. The correction covers every spec in one call. Writes `out/report.md` listing every player's mean and spread (BASE-03) and every claim, with stretch claims labelled "stretch" (EVAL-05), and links each chart in `charts` by its path under a "Cross-play" section (EVAL-11).
420	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if a spec names a player missing from `scores`
421	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.run_claims/ · **Cases:** a fake returning one passed and one failed Claim
422	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
423	
424	## viz
425	
426	### viz.Chart: type
427	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Chart: path: Path; groups: tuple[str, ...]; baselines: tuple[str, ...]`
428	- **Invariants:** `path` is a written PNG. `groups` and `baselines` list what the chart draws, in legend order.
429	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.Chart/ · **Cases:** the ladder chart (three agents and the reference, two baselines); the ablation chart
430	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
431	
432	### viz.plot_curves: Learning-curve chart
433	`def plot_curves(groups: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]], *, scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]], baselines: Sequence[str], out: Path) -> Chart`
434	- **Invariants:** one line per group (the mean over its seeds, with a min–max band). Each name in `baselines` gets a horizontal line at its pooled mean from `scores` (EVAL-06). Several groups on one chart form the ablation comparison (ABL-04).
435	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if a baseline name is missing from `scores`
436	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.plot_curves/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the ladder Chart
437	- **Used by:** lab; PL-04
438	
439	### viz.plot_crossplay: Cross-play heat map and ratings
440	`def plot_crossplay(table: CrossPlay, ratings: Ratings, *, title: str, label: str = "", out: Path) -> Chart`
441	- **Invariants:** one heat map per team (predator generation by prey generation) and a line chart of both teams' ratings by generation; `label` (such as "reported") is printed on the figure (EVAL-11).
442	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.plot_crossplay/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the rung-2 Chart
443	- **Used by:** lab; PL-06
444	
445	### viz.Replay: type
446	```python
447	class Replay:
448	    def __init__(self, game: GameRecording, *, fps: float = 8.0) -> None: ...
449	    def advance(self) -> int: ...
450	    def show(self) -> None: ...
451	```
452	- **Invariants:** each frame shows the board (the grid; or the tank with the ball, the fish, the predator, the prey and the pellets, caught animals greyed), the scores and the step (REPLAY-01). Space toggles pause, the right arrow calls `advance` once, and `+`/`-` double or halve `fps` (REPLAY-02). `advance` returns the new frame index and stops at the last frame.
453	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/viz.Replay/ · **Cases:** a replay double advancing a 12-frame game to its end
454	- **Used by:** lab; PL-05
455	
456	## lab
457	
458	### lab.main: Command line
459	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
460	- **Invariants:** subcommands are `train <settings.toml> [--seeds 0-4]`, `eval <run | random | scripted>… [--env snake|tank|predprey] [--team T]`, `replay <run> [--game N | --new --seed S | --pair <predator gen> <prey gen>]`, `crossplay <run>` and `report <report.toml>`. `train` builds the env and agent from the settings alone (RUN-01), and runs reference settings through `train_reference`. An ablation settings file carries its full agent's step budget, and `report` refuses an ablation whose seeds or steps differ from its full agent's (ABL-04). Returns 0 on success, 2 on bad input.
461	- **Errors:** bad settings and missing runs print the `ValueError` or `FileNotFoundError` message and return 2
462	- **Used by:** the learner; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06

303	+"""The two interfaces every environment and every player implements (AD-03, AD-09, AD-13)."""
304	+
305	+from collections.abc import Mapping
306	+from pathlib import Path
307	+from typing import Literal, Protocol
308	+
309	+import numpy as np
310	+
311	+from rllab.core.types import StepResult, TeamSpec, Transition
312	+
313	+
314	+class Env(Protocol):
315	+    """A game over teams of agents, each team batch-first."""
316	+
317	+    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
318	+    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
319	+
320	+    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
321	+
322	+    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult: ...
323	+
324	+    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray: ...
325	+
326	+
327	+class Agent(Protocol):
328	+    """A player acting for one team: one action per agent of the batch."""
329	+
330	+    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray: ...
331	+
332	+    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...
333	+
334	+    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
335	+
336	+    def load(self, path: Path) -> None: ...

364	+"""The core's data types: teams, actions, steps, transitions and run settings (AD-09, AD-13)."""
365	+
366	+from collections.abc import Mapping
367	+from dataclasses import dataclass
368	+from typing import Literal
369	+
370	+import numpy as np
371	+
372	+SOLO: str = "agents"
373	+"""The one team name every single-team environment uses."""
374	+
375	+
376	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
377	+class ActionSpace:
378	+    """Discrete: `n >= 2` and `dim == 0`. Continuous: `dim >= 1` and `n == 0`."""
379	+
380	+    kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]
381	+    n: int = 0
382	+    dim: int = 0
383	+
384	+
385	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
386	+class TeamSpec:
387	+    """One team's size, per-agent observation shape and shared action space."""
388	+
389	+    n_agents: int
390	+    obs_shape: tuple[int, ...]
391	+    actions: ActionSpace
392	+
393	+
394	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
395	+class StepResult:
396	+    """One environment step, each map keyed by team and batch-first over that team's agents."""
397	+
398	+    obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]
399	+    reward: dict[str, np.ndarray]
400	+    done: dict[str, np.ndarray]
401	+    terminated: bool
402	+    truncated: bool
403	+    info: dict[str, float]
404	+
405	+
406	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
407	+class Transition:
408	+    """One learning team's step, batch-first; `terminated` is never set by truncation."""
409	+
410	+    obs: np.ndarray
411	+    actions: np.ndarray
412	+    reward: np.ndarray
413	+    next_obs: np.ndarray
414	+    terminated: np.ndarray
415	+    active: np.ndarray
416	+    truncated: bool
417	+
418	+
419	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
420	+class Rewards:
421	+    """Reward sizes; for the corridor `food` is the goal reward."""
422	+
423	+    food: float = 1.0
424	+    death: float = -1.0
425	+    step: float = 0.0
426	+
427	+
428	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
429	+class Exploration:
430	+    """A rate falling linearly from `start` to `end` over `decay_steps`, then constant."""
431	+
432	+    start: float = 1.0
433	+    end: float = 0.05
434	+    decay_steps: int = 50_000
435	+
436	+
437	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
438	+class AgentParams:
439	+    """Learner hyperparameters; each agent reads the fields it uses."""
440	+
441	+    lr: float
442	+    gamma: float
443	+    batch_size: int = 64
444	+    memory_size: int = 50_000
445	+    target_every: int = 1_000
446	+    learn_start: int = 1_000
447	+    hidden: int = 128
448	+    net: Literal["mlp", "cnn"] = "mlp"
449	+    rollout_steps: int = 2048
450	+    epochs: int = 10
451	+    clip: float = 0.2
452	+    gae_lambda: float = 0.95
453	+    games_per_update: int = 10
454	+
455	+
456	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
457	+class RunSettings:
458	+    """Everything one run needs, fully resolved (RUN-01)."""
459	+
460	+    name: str
461	+    env: Literal["snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"]
462	+    view: Literal["compact", "grid", "index", "full", "limited"]
463	+    agent: Literal["tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference"]
464	+    n_agents: int
465	+    opponents: Mapping[str, Literal["learn", "scripted", "random"]]
466	+    self_play: Literal["none", "latest", "pool"]
467	+    pool_latest: float
468	+    generations: int
469	+    ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target", "no_explore"]
470	+    rewards: Rewards
471	+    exploration: Exploration
472	+    params: AgentParams
473	+    steps: int
474	+    seed: int
475	+    eval_every: int
476	+    eval_games: int

637	+"""What one evaluation asks for and what it returns."""
638	+
639	+from dataclasses import dataclass
640	+
641	+import numpy as np
642	+
643	+from rllab.core import SOLO
644	+from rllab.runs import GameRecording
645	+
646	+
647	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
648	+class EvalSpec:
649	+    """`n_games` games of `team` on `eval_seeds(train_seed, n_games)`, the first `record` recorded."""
650	+
651	+    player: str
652	+    train_seed: int
653	+    team: str = SOLO
654	+    n_games: int = 200
655	+    record: int = 0
656	+
657	+    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
658	+        if self.n_games < 1:
659	+            raise ValueError(f"n_games must be at least 1, got {self.n_games}")
660	+        if not 0 <= self.record <= self.n_games:
661	+            raise ValueError(f"record must be within 0..{self.n_games}, got {self.record}")
662	+
663	+
664	+@dataclass(frozen=True)
665	+class ScoreSet:
666	+    """One score per evaluation game, and the first recorded games."""
667	+
668	+    player: str
669	+    train_seed: int
670	+    scores: np.ndarray
671	+    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]

717	index 0000000..791d65b
718	--- /dev/null
719	+++ b/src/rllab/lab/cli.py
720	@@ -0,0 +1,93 @@
721	+"""The command line: `python -m rllab.lab <train | eval | replay | report> ...`."""
722	+
723	+import argparse
724	+import os
725	+import sys
726	+from collections.abc import Sequence
727	+from pathlib import Path
728	+
729	+from rllab.core import load_settings
730	+from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl01_train import run_train
731	+from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl02_train_reference import run_train_reference
732	+from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl03_evaluate import run_evaluate
733	+from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl04_report import run_report
734	+from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl05_replay import run_replay
735	+
736	+
737	+def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
738	+    """Run one subcommand; 0 on success, 2 on bad settings or a missing run."""
739	+    args = _parser().parse_args(argv)
740	+    if args.smoke:
741	+        os.environ["MPLBACKEND"] = "Agg"  # headless: charts are written, no window opens
742	+    try:
743	+        args.command(args)
744	+    except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
745	+        print(f"error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
746	+        return 2
747	+    return 0
748	+
749	+
750	+def _train(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
751	+    """PL-01 per seed, or PL-02 when the settings name the reference agent."""
752	+    path = Path(args.settings)
753	+    settings = load_settings(path)
754	+    entry = run_train_reference if settings.agent == "reference" else run_train
755	+    for seed in seed_range(args.seeds, default=settings.seed):
756	+        entry(path, seed)
757	+
758	+
759	+def _eval(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
760	+    for target in args.targets:
761	+        run_evaluate(target, args.seed)
762	+
763	+
764	+def _replay(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
765	+    if args.new and args.seed is None:
766	+        raise ValueError("--new needs --seed S")
767	+    run_replay(Path(args.run), game=args.game, new_seed=args.seed if args.new else None)
768	+
769	+
770	+def _report(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
771	+    spec = Path(args.spec)
772	+    run_report(spec, out=spec.parent)
773	+
774	+
775	+def seed_range(text: str | None, *, default: int) -> list[int]:
776	+    """`"0-4"` is seeds 0 to 4, `"3"` is seed 3, and no text is `default`."""
777	+    if text is None:
778	+        return [default]
779	+    first, _, last = text.partition("-")
780	+    seeds = list(range(int(first), int(last or first) + 1))
781	+    if not seeds:
782	+        raise ValueError(f"--seeds {text!r} names no seed")
783	+    return seeds
784	+
785	+
786	+def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
787	+    common = argparse.ArgumentParser(add_help=False)
788	+    common.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="headless smoke run")
789	+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m rllab.lab")
790	+    commands = parser.add_subparsers(required=True)
791	+
792	+    train = commands.add_parser("train", parents=[common], help="train one run per seed")
793	+    train.add_argument("settings", help="settings TOML file")
794	+    train.add_argument("--seeds", help="seeds, such as 0-4 (default: the settings' seed)")
795	+    train.set_defaults(command=_train)
796	+
797	+    evaluate = commands.add_parser("eval", parents=[common], help="score players")
798	+    evaluate.add_argument("targets", nargs="+", help="run folders, random or scripted")
799	+    evaluate.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0, help="training seed to score on")
800	+    evaluate.set_defaults(command=_eval)
801	+
802	+    replay = commands.add_parser("replay", parents=[common], help="replay a recorded game")
803	+    replay.add_argument("run", help="run folder")
804	+    which = replay.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
805	+    which.add_argument("--game", type=int, default=0, help="recorded game index")
806	+    which.add_argument("--new", action="store_true", help="play a new game on --seed")
807	+    replay.add_argument("--seed", type=int, help="seed of the new game")
808	+    replay.set_defaults(command=_replay)
809	+
810	+    report = commands.add_parser("report", parents=[common], help="test claims, draw charts")
811	+    report.add_argument("spec", help="report TOML file; outputs go beside it")
812	+    report.set_defaults(command=_report)
813	+    return parser

818	+++ b/src/rllab/lab/pipelines/__init__.py

5	## PL-01: Train a run
6	**Entry:** `src/rllab/lab/pipelines/pl01_train.py::run_train` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab.lab train <settings.toml> --seeds 0-4`
7	**Covers:** SNAKE-01–SNAKE-09, FISH-01–FISH-10, HUNT-01–HUNT-09, CORE-01–CORE-07, AGENT-01–AGENT-06, PG-01–PG-05, SELF-01–SELF-04, BASE-06–BASE-08, ABL-01–ABL-03, RUN-01–RUN-04, DATA-02, QR-01–QR-03, QR-05–QR-07
8	
9	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
10	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
11	| 1 | `load_settings` | core.settings | path → RunSettings | TOML into the frozen dataclasses; unknown names raise `ValueError` naming the key | RUN-01 |
12	| 2 | `make_env` | lab.build | RunSettings → Env | `if` on `settings.env`: Snake, Corridor, FishTank (with `n_agents` fish) or PredatorPrey, returned as a zero-argument factory | CORE-01, DATA-02 |
13	| 3 | `Snake` | envs.snake | view, Rewards → Env | full rules on 10×10, compact and grid views | SNAKE-01–SNAKE-09 |
14	| 4 | `Corridor` | envs.corridor | length, Rewards → Env | 10 cells, one-hot position, 50-step cap | CORE-03 |
15	| 5 | `FishTank` | envs.tank | view, n_fish, Rewards → Env | vectorised ball reflection, shortened swim vectors, wall stop, catches, 1,000-step cap, full view (AD-12) | FISH-01–FISH-09 |
16	| 6 | `PredatorPrey` | envs.predprey | Rewards → Env | the tank's movement functions (AD-16) plus pellets, catches, 50-step prey respawns, both teams' limited views, 1,000-step cap | HUNT-01–HUNT-09 |
17	| 7 | `make_agent` | lab.build | RunSettings, Env → Agent | one agent per team of `env.teams`: learners from `settings.agent` (tabular, dqn, reinforce or ppo) sized from the team's `TeamSpec`, other teams scripted or random per `settings.opponents`; ablation switches from `settings.ablation` | ABL-01–ABL-03, PG-05, HUNT-07, SELF-02 |
18	| 8 | `TabularAgent` | agents.tabular | AgentParams, Exploration → Agent | dict of NumPy rows keyed on observation bytes; linear exploration decay | AGENT-01, AGENT-02, AGENT-06, ABL-03 |
19	| 9 | `DQNAgent` | agents.dqn | AgentParams, Exploration → Agent | 128×128 MLP, uniform memory, hard target refresh, Huber loss, gradient clip 10 | AGENT-02–AGENT-06, ABL-01, ABL-02 |
20	| 10 | `ReinforceAgent` | agents.reinforce | ActionSpace, AgentParams → Agent | Gaussian 64×64 Tanh policy with a learned log-std; one update per `games_per_update` games on normalised returns-to-go (AD-11) | PG-01, PG-02, AGENT-06 |
21	| 11 | `PPOAgent` | agents.ppo | ActionSpace, AgentParams → Agent | the same policy plus a 64×64 value net; GAE, clipped objective, `epochs` passes over minibatches, one instance for every fish (AD-11) | PG-01, PG-03, PG-04, AGENT-06, SELF-01 |
22	| 12 | `ScriptedPredator` | baselines.scripted_predator | () → Agent | length-1 vector toward the nearest visible prey, else toward the centre | BASE-06 |
23	| 13 | `ScriptedPrey` | baselines.scripted_prey | () → Agent | flee within 0.3, else toward the nearest visible pellet, else still | BASE-07 |
24	| 14 | `RandomSwimmer` | baselines.random_swimmer | n_agents, seed → Agent | uniform over the unit disc, for either team | BASE-08 |
25	| 15 | `train` | train.loop | Env, Agent, RunSettings → RunRecord | batch-first step loop over every team, building one Transition per learning team and calling its `learn` after each step; each learner's generation every tenth of the run (SELF-04); `evaluate.evaluate` every `eval_every` into a CurvePoint; progress line every 30 s; checkpoints and 5 recorded games at the end | CORE-02, CORE-07, RUN-02–RUN-04, SELF-01, SELF-04, QR-01–QR-03, QR-07 |
26	| 16 | `OpponentPool` | selfplay.pool | Agent, latest_share, seed → OpponentPool | always the latest policy (rung 2's behaviour) | SELF-03 |
27	
28	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
29	```python
30	def run_train(settings_path: Path, seed: int) -> RunRecord:
31	    settings = load_settings(settings_path)
32	    settings = with_seed(settings, seed)
33	    make = make_env(settings)
34	    agents = make_agent(settings, make())
35	    return train(make, agents, settings)
36	```

56	**Entry:** `src/rllab/lab/pipelines/pl03_evaluate.py::run_evaluate` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab.lab eval <run folder | random | scripted> [--env snake|tank|predprey] [--team T] [--vs scripted|random|<run folder>] [--seed S]`
57	**Covers:** EVAL-01, DATA-01, BASE-01–BASE-08, AGENT-07, RUN-05, SELF-07, QR-04
58	
59	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
60	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
61	| 1 | `load_player` | lab.players | target, seed, env, team, vs → Agent, RunSettings | one agent per team: `team`'s from the run folder (`runs.open_run`, `make_env` and `make_agent` as PL-01 built them, then `Agent.load` of that team's checkpoint) or the named baseline; every other team from `vs` (scripted, random, or a run folder's final policy: the SELF-07 panel); single-team envs ignore `team` and `vs` | RUN-05, SELF-07 |
62	| 2 | `RandomPlayer` | baselines.random | n_actions, seed → Agent | uniform choice from a seeded generator | BASE-01 |
63	| 3 | `ScriptedPlayer` | baselines.scripted | () → Agent | toward food if safe, else any safe move, else straight | BASE-02 |
64	| 4 | `RandomSwimmer` | baselines.random_swimmer | n_fish, seed → Agent | uniform over the unit disc from a seeded generator | BASE-04 |
65	| 5 | `ScriptedFish` | baselines.scripted_fish | () → Agent | length-1 vector away from the ball's centre on the full view | BASE-05 |
66	| 6 | `ScriptedPredator` | baselines.scripted_predator | () → Agent | PL-01 as built | BASE-06 |
67	| 7 | `ScriptedPrey` | baselines.scripted_prey | () → Agent | PL-01 as built | BASE-07 |
68	| 8 | `ReferencePlayer` | reference.player | checkpoint → Agent | load the library model; deterministic `predict` | REF-01 |
69	| 9 | `make_env` | lab.build | RunSettings → Env | PL-01 as built | CORE-01 |
70	| 10 | `evaluate` | evaluate.protocol | Env, Agent, train_seed, team → ScoreSet | 200 games on `eval_seeds(train_seed, 200)`, `explore=False`, no `learn` calls, scoring `team` | EVAL-01, DATA-01, AGENT-07, QR-04 |
71	| 11 | `print_scores` | lab.printing | ScoreSet → ScoreSet | prints mean, standard deviation, min and max on one line; returns its input | EVAL-01, BASE-03 |
72	
73	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
74	```python
75	def run_evaluate(target: str, seed: int, env: str = "snake", team: str = SOLO, vs: str = "scripted") -> ScoreSet:
76	    agents, settings = load_player(target, seed, env, team, vs)
77	    make = make_env(settings)
78	    scores = evaluate(make, agents, team=team, player=target, train_seed=seed)
79	    return print_scores(scores)
80	```

83	**Entry:** `src/rllab/lab/pipelines/pl04_report.py::run_report` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab.lab report <report.toml>`
84	**Covers:** EVAL-02–EVAL-11, REF-02, REF-03, ABL-04, BASE-03
85	
86	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
87	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
88	| 1 | `load_report_spec` | lab.report_spec | path → ClaimSpec, targets | TOML: players (name → run folders or a baseline name, plus `team` and `vs` for predator-prey), claims, two curve charts, and PL-06's cross-play chart paths | EVAL-02–EVAL-05, EVAL-09–EVAL-11 |
89	| 2 | `score_players` | lab.players | targets → ScoreSet | for each player, opponent and seed: PL-03 steps 1, 9 and 10 as built; a predator-prey player's panel scores are pooled over its `vs` opponents | EVAL-01, BASE-03, SELF-07 |
90	| 3 | `open_curve_runs` | lab.players | targets → RunRecord | `runs.open_run` per folder; `ValueError` if an ablation's seeds or `steps` differ from its full agent's | ABL-04, RUN-05 |
91	| 4 | `run_claims` | compare.claims | ScoreSet, ClaimSpec → Claim | each claim through the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill, one correction over all claims; REF-02's ratio as arithmetic; writes `report.md`, linking the cross-play charts by path | EVAL-02–EVAL-05, EVAL-07–EVAL-11, REF-02, REF-03, BASE-03 |
92	| 5 | `plot_curves` | viz.curves | RunRecord, ScoreSet → Chart | mean over seeds with a min–max band; one horizontal line at each named baseline's pooled mean; once for the ladder chart and once for the ablation chart | EVAL-06, ABL-04 |
93	
94	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
95	```python
96	def run_report(spec_path: Path, out: Path) -> list[Claim]:
97	    specs, targets = load_report_spec(spec_path)
98	    scores = score_players(targets)
99	    curves = open_curve_runs(targets)
100	    claims = run_claims(scores, specs, charts=targets.crossplay, out=out)
101	    plot_curves(curves.ladder, scores=scores, baselines=targets.baselines, out=out / "ladder.png")
102	    plot_curves(curves.ablations, scores=scores, baselines=targets.baselines, out=out / "ablations.png")
103	    return claims
104	```

107	**Entry:** `src/rllab/lab/pipelines/pl05_replay.py::run_replay` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab.lab replay <run folder> [--game N | --new --seed S | --pair <predator gen> <prey gen>]`
108	**Covers:** REPLAY-01–REPLAY-03
109	
110	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
111	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
112	| 1 | `open_run` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | read `settings.json` and `games.npz`; `FileNotFoundError` naming the run | REPLAY-01, RUN-05 |
113	| 2 | `pick_game` | lab.replay | RunRecord, game, new_seed, pair → GameRecording | the recorded game at index N; with `--pair`, one new game of those two generations (`lab.players.load_generation`, then PL-03 step 10 as built); with `--new`, PL-03 steps 1, 9 and 10 as built with `n_games=1, record=1` | REPLAY-01, REPLAY-03 |
114	| 3 | `Replay` | viz.replay | GameRecording → Replay | Matplotlib loop drawing grid cells (`imshow`) or tank circles by `board` (AD-12); space, right arrow, `+`/`-` key handlers; `show()` blocks until the window closes | REPLAY-01, REPLAY-02 |
115	
116	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
117	```python
118	def run_replay(run: Path, game: int = 0, new_seed: int | None = None, pair: tuple[int, int] | None = None) -> None:
119	    record = open_run(run)
120	    recording = pick_game(record, game, new_seed, pair)
121	    Replay(recording).show()
122	```

124	## PL-06: Cross-play
125	**Entry:** `src/rllab/lab/pipelines/pl06_crossplay.py::run_crossplay` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab.lab crossplay <run folder> [--label reported]`
126	**Covers:** SELF-05, SELF-06, EVAL-11
127	
128	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
129	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
130	| 1 | `open_run` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | PL-05 as built | RUN-05 |
131	| 2 | `make_env` | lab.build | RunSettings → Env | PL-01 as built | CORE-01 |
132	| 3 | `cross_play` | selfplay.crossplay | RunRecord, Env → CrossPlay | every predator generation against every prey generation, 50 games each on `eval_seeds`, `explore=False`, generations loaded with `lab.players.load_generation` | SELF-05 |
133	| 4 | `ratings` | selfplay.ratings | CrossPlay → Ratings | row means for the predator, column means for the prey | SELF-06 |
134	| 5 | `plot_crossplay` | viz.crossplay | CrossPlay, Ratings → Chart | two heat maps and a ratings-by-generation line chart, with the label printed | EVAL-11 |
135	
136	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
137	```python
138	def run_crossplay(run: Path, label: str, out: Path) -> Chart:
139	    record = open_run(run)
140	    make = make_env(record.settings)
141	    table = cross_play(record, make, load_generation)
142	    scores = ratings(table)
143	    return plot_crossplay(table, scores, title=record.settings.name, label=label, out=out)
144	```
