87	class ContractFunctions(Protocol):
88	    """Each method's signature, minus `self`, is the frozen one of the same-named function."""
89	
90	    def load_settings(self, path: Path) -> RunSettings: ...
91	    def create_run(self, settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunWriter: ...
92	    def open_run(self, path: Path) -> RunRecord: ...
93	    def eval_seeds(self, train_seed: int, n: int) -> list[int]: ...
94	    def evaluate(
95	        self, make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], spec: EvalSpec
96	    ) -> ScoreSet: ...
97	    def train(
98	        self,
99	        make_env: Callable[[], Env],
100	        agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
101	        settings: RunSettings,
102	        *,
103	        root: Path = Path("runs"),
104	    ) -> RunRecord: ...
105	    def train_reference(self, settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord: ...
106	    def run_claims(
107	        self,
108	        scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]],
109	        specs: Sequence[ClaimSpec],
110	        *,
111	        alpha: float = 0.05,
112	        charts: Sequence[Chart] = (),
113	        out: Path,
114	    ) -> list[Claim]: ...
115	    def plot_curves(
116	        self,
117	        groups: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]],
118	        *,
119	        scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]],
120	        baselines: Sequence[str],
121	        out: Path,
122	    ) -> Chart: ...
123	    def main(self, argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int: ...
124	
125	
126	class StepFunctions(Protocol):
127	    """The step and entry functions outside the contract, by the same rule."""
128	
129	    def with_seed(self, settings: RunSettings, seed: int) -> RunSettings: ...
130	    def make_env(self, settings: RunSettings) -> Callable[[], Env]: ...
131	    def make_agent(self, settings: RunSettings, env: Env) -> dict[str, Agent]: ...
132	    def load_player(self, target: str, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, Agent], RunSettings]: ...
133	    def score_players(self, targets: ReportTargets) -> dict[str, tuple[ScoreSet, ...]]: ...
134	    def open_curve_runs(self, targets: ReportTargets) -> CurveRuns: ...
135	    def load_report_spec(self, path: Path) -> tuple[tuple[ClaimSpec, ...], ReportTargets]: ...
136	    def pick_game(self, record: RunRecord, game: int, new_seed: int | None) -> GameRecording: ...
137	    def print_scores(self, scores: ScoreSet) -> ScoreSet: ...
138	    def run_train(self, settings_path: Path, seed: int) -> RunRecord: ...
139	    def run_train_reference(self, settings_path: Path, seed: int) -> RunRecord: ...
140	    def run_evaluate(self, target: str, seed: int) -> ScoreSet: ...
141	    def run_report(self, spec_path: Path, out: Path) -> list[Claim]: ...
142	    def run_replay(self, run: Path, game: int = 0, new_seed: int | None = None) -> None: ...

282	def _without_self(function: Callable[..., object]) -> inspect.Signature:
283	    signature = inspect.signature(function)
284	    return signature.replace(parameters=list(signature.parameters.values())[1:])

1	"""Contract samples as typed values: load, check against a schema, build, encode.
2	
3	Each `tests/contracts/<component>.<Symbol>/` folder holds `schema.json` and `samples.json`
4	(case name -> JSON value), and for behaviour contracts a `fake.py` whose doubles and
5	`check_*` functions any test can load with `fake(symbol)`. Load this module by path:
6	
7	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("contracts_codec", ROOT / "codec.py")
8	
9	Arrays are encoded as `{"dtype": ..., "shape": [...], "data": nested lists}`.
10	The schema language is a small JSON Schema subset: type, const, enum, properties, required,
11	additionalProperties, items, minItems, maxItems, minimum, maximum, exclusiveMinimum, anyOf.
12	"""
13	
14	from __future__ import annotations
15	
16	import collections.abc
17	import importlib.util
18	import json
19	import re
20	import sys
21	import types
22	from dataclasses import fields, is_dataclass
23	from pathlib import Path
24	from typing import Any, Literal, Union, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints
25	
26	import numpy as np
27	
28	ROOT = Path(__file__).parent
29	_JSON_TYPES: dict[str, tuple[type, ...]] = {
30	    "object": (dict,),
31	    "array": (list,),
32	    "string": (str,),
33	    "integer": (int,),
34	    "number": (int, float),
35	    "boolean": (bool,),
36	    "null": (type(None),),
37	}
38	
39	
40	# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- files
41	def load_json(symbol: str, name: str) -> Any:
42	    return json.loads((ROOT / symbol / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
43	
44	
45	def samples(symbol: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
46	    """The folder's samples, case name to JSON value."""
47	    return load_json(symbol, "samples.json")
48	
49	
50	def schema(symbol: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
51	    return load_json(symbol, "schema.json")
52	
53	
54	def sample(symbol: str, case: str, hint: Any) -> Any:
55	    """One sample built as `hint`."""
56	    return build(hint, samples(symbol)[case])
57	
58	
59	def load_module(path: Path) -> types.ModuleType:
60	    """Import a contract folder's Python file by path (the folders are not packages)."""
61	    name = "contracts_" + re.sub(r"\W", "_", str(path.relative_to(ROOT).with_suffix("")))
62	    if name in sys.modules:
63	        return sys.modules[name]
64	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
65	    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
66	        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {path}")
67	    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
68	    sys.modules[name] = module
69	    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
70	    return module
71	
72	
73	def fake(symbol: str) -> types.ModuleType:
74	    """The folder's `fake.py`: its doubles and its `check_*` functions."""
75	    return load_module(ROOT / symbol / "fake.py")
76	
77	
78	# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ schema
79	def schema_errors(rule: dict[str, Any], value: Any, where: str = "$") -> list[str]:
80	    """Every way `value` breaks `rule`, as `path: message` lines (empty when it conforms)."""
81	    if "anyOf" in rule:
82	        if any(not schema_errors(option, value, where) for option in rule["anyOf"]):
83	            return []
84	        return [f"{where}: matches no anyOf option"]
85	    errors = _scalar_errors(rule, value, where)
86	    if errors:
87	        return errors
88	    if isinstance(value, dict):
89	        errors += _object_errors(rule, value, where)
90	    if isinstance(value, list):
91	        errors += _array_errors(rule, value, where)
92	    return errors
93	
94	
95	def _type_ok(name: str, value: Any) -> bool:
96	    if isinstance(value, bool) and name in {"integer", "number"}:
97	        return False
98	    return isinstance(value, _JSON_TYPES[name])
99	
100	
101	def _scalar_errors(rule: dict[str, Any], value: Any, where: str) -> list[str]:
102	    kinds = rule.get("type")
103	    names = [kinds] if isinstance(kinds, str) else kinds
104	    errors = []
105	    if names is not None and not any(_type_ok(n, value) for n in names):
106	        errors.append(f"{where}: expected {kinds}, got {type(value).__name__}")
107	    elif "const" in rule and value != rule["const"]:
108	        errors.append(f"{where}: expected {rule['const']!r}, got {value!r}")
109	    elif "enum" in rule and value not in rule["enum"]:
110	        errors.append(f"{where}: {value!r} not one of {rule['enum']}")
111	    elif isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool):
112	        errors += _range_errors(rule, value, where)
113	    return errors
114	
115	
116	def _range_errors(rule: dict[str, Any], value: float, where: str) -> list[str]:
117	    errors = []
118	    if "minimum" in rule and value < rule["minimum"]:
119	        errors.append(f"{where}: {value} below {rule['minimum']}")
120	    if "maximum" in rule and value > rule["maximum"]:
121	        errors.append(f"{where}: {value} above {rule['maximum']}")
122	    if "exclusiveMinimum" in rule and value <= rule["exclusiveMinimum"]:
123	        errors.append(f"{where}: {value} not above {rule['exclusiveMinimum']}")
124	    return errors
125	
126	
127	def _object_errors(rule: dict[str, Any], value: dict[str, Any], where: str) -> list[str]:
128	    errors = [f"{where}: missing {key!r}" for key in rule.get("required", []) if key not in value]
129	    known = rule.get("properties", {})
130	    extra = rule.get("additionalProperties", True)
131	    for key, item in value.items():
132	        if key in known:
133	            errors += schema_errors(known[key], item, f"{where}.{key}")
134	        elif extra is False:
135	            errors.append(f"{where}: unexpected {key!r}")
136	        elif isinstance(extra, dict):
137	            errors += schema_errors(extra, item, f"{where}.{key}")
138	    return errors
139	
140	
141	def _array_errors(rule: dict[str, Any], value: list[Any], where: str) -> list[str]:
142	    errors = []
143	    if "minItems" in rule and len(value) < rule["minItems"]:
144	        errors.append(f"{where}: {len(value)} items, fewer than {rule['minItems']}")
145	    if "maxItems" in rule and len(value) > rule["maxItems"]:
146	        errors.append(f"{where}: {len(value)} items, more than {rule['maxItems']}")
147	    if "items" in rule:
148	        for i, item in enumerate(value):
149	            errors += schema_errors(rule["items"], item, f"{where}[{i}]")
150	    return errors
151	
152	
153	def assert_samples_valid(symbol: str) -> None:
154	    """Every sample in the folder conforms to the folder's schema."""
155	    rule = schema(symbol)
156	    found = {case: schema_errors(rule, value) for case, value in samples(symbol).items()}
157	    assert found, f"{symbol} has no samples"
158	    assert {case: errs for case, errs in found.items() if errs} == {}
159	
160	
161	# ------------------------------------------------------------------- typed values
162	def build(hint: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
163	    """`data` (decoded JSON) as a value of type `hint`."""
164	    if is_dataclass(hint) and isinstance(hint, type):
165	        return _build_dataclass(hint, data)
166	    if hint is np.ndarray:
167	        return _build_array(data)
168	    if get_origin(hint) is not None:
169	        return _build_generic(hint, data)
170	    return _build_scalar(hint, data)
171	
172	
173	def _build_generic(hint: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
174	    origin = get_origin(hint)
175	    if origin is Literal:
176	        if data not in get_args(hint):
177	            raise ValueError(f"{data!r} is not one of {get_args(hint)}")
178	        return data
179	    if origin in {Union, types.UnionType}:
180	        return _build_union(hint, data)
181	    if origin is tuple:
182	        return _build_tuple(hint, data)
183	    if origin in {dict, collections.abc.Mapping}:
184	        value_hint = get_args(hint)[1]
185	        return {key: build(value_hint, item) for key, item in data.items()}
186	    raise TypeError(f"cannot build {hint}")
187	
188	
189	def _build_dataclass(cls: type, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
190	    hints = get_type_hints(cls)
191	    names = {f.name for f in fields(cls)}
192	    unknown = set(data) - names
193	    if unknown:
194	        raise ValueError(f"{cls.__name__} has no fields {sorted(unknown)}")
195	    return cls(**{name: build(hints[name], data[name]) for name in names if name in data})
196	
197	
198	def _build_array(data: dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:
199	    array = np.asarray(data["data"], dtype=data["dtype"])
200	    if array.size == 0:
201	        array = array.reshape(data["shape"])
202	    if list(array.shape) != list(data["shape"]):
203	        raise ValueError(f"array shape {array.shape} is not {data['shape']}")
204	    return array
205	
206	
207	def _build_union(hint: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
208	    options = [a for a in get_args(hint) if a is not type(None)]
209	    if data is None and len(options) < len(get_args(hint)):
210	        return None
211	    if len(options) != 1:
212	        raise TypeError(f"cannot build the union {hint}")
213	    return build(options[0], data)
214	
215	
216	def _build_tuple(hint: Any, data: list[Any]) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
217	    args = get_args(hint)
218	    if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
219	        return tuple(build(args[0], item) for item in data)
220	    if len(args) != len(data):
221	        raise ValueError(f"expected {len(args)} items, got {len(data)}")
222	    return tuple(build(a, item) for a, item in zip(args, data, strict=True))
223	
224	
225	def _build_scalar(hint: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
226	    if hint is Any:
227	        return data
228	    if hint is Path:
229	        return Path(data)
230	    if hint is float and isinstance(data, (int, float)) and not isinstance(data, bool):
231	        return float(data)
232	    if hint in {int, str, bool} and type(data) is hint:
233	        return data
234	    raise TypeError(f"cannot build {hint} from {data!r}")
235	
236	
237	def encode(value: Any) -> Any:
238	    """A typed value as JSON: the inverse of `build`."""
239	    if is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
240	        return {f.name: encode(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in fields(value)}
241	    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
242	        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
243	    if isinstance(value, collections.abc.Mapping):
244	        return {str(key): encode(item) for key, item in value.items()}
245	    if isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
246	        return [encode(item) for item in value]
247	    return _encode_scalar(value)
248	
249	
250	def _encode_scalar(value: Any) -> Any:
251	    if isinstance(value, Path):
252	        return str(value)
253	    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
254	        return value.item()
255	    return value

1	"""core.Env: a replay double serving a recorded game, and the check any Env must pass.
2	
3	`check_env(env, actions, seed=...)` takes the provider, so the real environments run the
4	same check as the double.
5	"""
6	
7	from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
8	from dataclasses import dataclass
9	from typing import Literal
10	
11	import numpy as np
12	import pytest
13	
14	from rllab.core import Env, StepResult, TeamSpec
15	
16	GRID_CODES = {0, 1, 2, 3}  # empty, head, body, food or goal
17	
18	
19	@dataclass(frozen=True)
20	class EnvRecording:
21	    """One recorded game: its teams, the reset, then each step's actions, result and frame."""
22	
23	    teams: dict[str, TeamSpec]
24	    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
25	    seed: int
26	    obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]
27	    info: dict[str, float]
28	    start_frame: np.ndarray
29	    actions: tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], ...]
30	    results: tuple[StepResult, ...]
31	    frames: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]
32	
33	
34	class ReplayEnv(Env):
35	    """Serves a recording: `reset` rewinds to its start, `step` returns the next result.
36	
37	    `step` validates the actions as the contract says (`ValueError`) and raises `RuntimeError`
38	    once the recorded game is over or before the first `reset`.
39	    """
40	
41	    def __init__(self, recording: EnvRecording) -> None:
42	        self.teams = recording.teams
43	        self.board = recording.board
44	        self.recording = recording
45	        self._t = -1
46	
47	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
48	        self._t = 0
49	        return {k: v.copy() for k, v in self.recording.obs.items()}, dict(self.recording.info)
50	
51	    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult:
52	        if not 0 <= self._t < len(self.recording.results):
53	            raise RuntimeError("step called while no game is running")
54	        check_actions(self.teams, actions)
55	        result = self.recording.results[self._t]
56	        self._t += 1
57	        return result
58	
59	    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
60	        if self._t <= 0:
61	            return self.recording.start_frame.copy()
62	        return self.recording.frames[self._t - 1].copy()
63	
64	
65	def check_actions(teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec], actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> None:
66	    """`ValueError` for a missing team, a wrong shape, an out-of-range or non-finite action."""
67	    for team, spec in teams.items():
68	        if team not in actions:
69	            raise ValueError(f"no actions for team {team!r}")
70	        action = np.asarray(actions[team])
71	        space = spec.actions
72	        shape = (spec.n_agents,) if space.kind == "discrete" else (spec.n_agents, space.dim)
73	        if action.shape != shape:
74	            raise ValueError(f"team {team!r}: actions shaped {action.shape}, expected {shape}")
75	        if not np.all(np.isfinite(action)):
76	            raise ValueError(f"team {team!r}: non-finite action")
77	        if space.kind == "discrete" and not np.all((action >= 0) & (action < space.n)):
78	            raise ValueError(f"team {team!r}: action outside 0..{space.n - 1}")
79	
80	
81	def check_env(env: Env, actions: Sequence[Mapping[str, np.ndarray]], *, seed: int) -> None:
82	    """The core.Env contract on one game: shapes, dtypes, info keys, frames and errors."""
83	    obs, info = env.reset(seed)
84	    _assert_obs(env.teams, obs)
85	    _assert_info(env.teams, info)
86	    _assert_frame(env.board, env.frame())
87	    over = False
88	    for step_actions in actions:
89	        result = env.step(step_actions)
90	        _assert_result(env.teams, result)
91	        _assert_frame(env.board, env.frame())
92	        over = result.terminated or result.truncated
93	        if over:
94	            break
95	    if over:
96	        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
97	            env.step(actions[-1])
98	    env.reset(seed)
99	    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="team"):
100	        env.step({})
101	
102	
103	def _assert_obs(teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec], obs: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> None:
104	    assert set(obs) == set(teams)
105	    for team, spec in teams.items():
106	        assert obs[team].dtype == np.float32
107	        assert obs[team].shape == (spec.n_agents, *spec.obs_shape)
108	
109	
110	def _assert_info(teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec], info: Mapping[str, float]) -> None:
111	    expected = {"steps", "score", *(f"score:{team}" for team in teams)}
112	    assert expected <= set(info)
113	    assert info["score"] == info[f"score:{next(iter(teams))}"]
114	
115	
116	def _assert_result(teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec], result: StepResult) -> None:
117	    assert isinstance(result, StepResult)
118	    _assert_obs(teams, result.obs)
119	    _assert_info(teams, result.info)
120	    assert not (result.terminated and result.truncated)
121	    for team, spec in teams.items():
122	        assert result.reward[team].dtype == np.float32
123	        assert result.reward[team].shape == (spec.n_agents,)
124	        assert result.done[team].dtype == np.bool_
125	        assert result.done[team].shape == (spec.n_agents,)
126	
127	
128	def _assert_frame(board: str, frame: np.ndarray) -> None:

{
  "file_path": "/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r2/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-aea15fc6c8655553e/tests/contracts/core.Agent/fake.py"

1	"""core.Agent: a replay double serving recorded actions, and the check any Agent must pass.
2	
3	`check_agent(agent, recording, tmp)` takes the provider, so the real agents and baselines run
4	the same check as the double.
5	"""
6	
7	import json
8	from dataclasses import dataclass
9	from pathlib import Path
10	from typing import Literal
11	
12	import numpy as np
13	
14	from rllab.core import Agent, TeamSpec, Transition
15	
16	
17	@dataclass(frozen=True)
18	class AgentCall:
19	    """One recorded call: `act` (obs, explore, the action returned) or `learn` (a transition)."""
20	
21	    kind: Literal["act", "learn"]
22	    obs: np.ndarray | None = None
23	    explore: bool | None = None
24	    action: np.ndarray | None = None
25	    transition: Transition | None = None
26	
27	
28	@dataclass(frozen=True)
29	class AgentRecording:
30	    """The calls one agent received, acting for `team`."""
31	
32	    team: TeamSpec
33	    calls: tuple[AgentCall, ...]
34	
35	    def acts(self) -> list[AgentCall]:
36	        return [call for call in self.calls if call.kind == "act"]
37	
38	
39	class ReplayAgent(Agent):
40	    """Returns the recorded actions in order, starting again after the last.
41	
42	    Keeps what it was shown (`observed`) and taught (`learned`) for the consumer's test;
43	    `act` raises `ValueError` for a batch not shaped like the recording's team.
44	    """
45	
46	    def __init__(self, recording: AgentRecording) -> None:
47	        self.recording = recording
48	        self.observed: list[tuple[np.ndarray, bool]] = []
49	        self.learned: list[Transition] = []
50	        self._actions = [call.action for call in recording.acts() if call.action is not None]
51	        self._next = 0
52	
53	    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
54	        team = self.recording.team
55	        if obs.shape != (team.n_agents, *team.obs_shape):
56	            raise ValueError(f"observation shaped {obs.shape} for team {team}")
57	        self.observed.append((obs.copy(), explore))
58	        action = self._actions[self._next % len(self._actions)]
59	        self._next += 1
60	        return action.copy()
61	
62	    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
63	        self.learned.append(t)
64	
65	    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
66	        path.write_text(json.dumps({"next": self._next}), encoding="utf-8")
67	
68	    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
69	        self._next = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["next"]
70	
71	
72	def check_agent(agent: Agent, recording: AgentRecording, tmp: Path) -> None:
73	    """The core.Agent contract on the recorded calls: action shapes, `learn`, save then load."""
74	    for call in recording.calls:
75	        if call.kind == "act":
76	            assert call.obs is not None
77	            assert call.explore is not None
78	            assert_actions(recording.team, agent.act(call.obs, explore=call.explore))
79	        else:
80	            assert call.transition is not None
81	            agent.learn(call.transition)
82	    first = recording.acts()[0].obs
83	    assert first is not None
84	    checkpoint = tmp / "checkpoint"
85	    agent.save(checkpoint)
86	    before = agent.act(first, explore=False)
87	    agent.load(checkpoint)
88	    np.testing.assert_array_equal(agent.act(first, explore=False), before)
89	
90	
91	def assert_actions(team: TeamSpec, actions: np.ndarray) -> None:
92	    """One action per agent, shaped and ranged by the team's action space."""
93	    space = team.actions
94	    if space.kind == "discrete":
95	        assert actions.shape == (team.n_agents,)
96	        assert np.issubdtype(actions.dtype, np.integer)
97	        assert np.all((actions >= 0) & (actions < space.n))
98	    else:
99	        assert actions.shape == (team.n_agents, space.dim)
100	        assert np.issubdtype(actions.dtype, np.floating)
101

1	"""evaluate.evaluate: a fake serving the scripted-player ScoreSet, and the provider check.
2	
3	The fake echoes the sample's scores (repeated to `n_games`) under the spec's player and seed,
4	with `record` copies of the given recording. It never touches the env or the agents.
5	`check_evaluate` takes the provider.
6	"""
7	
8	from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
9	
10	import numpy as np
11	
12	from rllab.core import Agent, Env
13	from rllab.evaluate import EvalSpec, ScoreSet
14	from rllab.runs import GameRecording
15	
16	Evaluate = Callable[[Callable[[], Env], Mapping[str, Agent], EvalSpec], ScoreSet]
17	
18	
19	def make_evaluate(scores: ScoreSet, game: GameRecording) -> Evaluate:
20	    """An `evaluate` returning `scores`' values and `game` recordings."""
21	
22	    def evaluate(
23	        make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], spec: EvalSpec
24	    ) -> ScoreSet:
25	        return ScoreSet(
26	            player=spec.player,
27	            train_seed=spec.train_seed,
28	            scores=np.resize(scores.scores, spec.n_games),
29	            games=(game,) * spec.record,
30	        )
31	
32	    return evaluate
33	
34	
35	def check_evaluate(
36	    evaluate: Evaluate,
37	    make_env: Callable[[], Env],
38	    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
39	    spec: EvalSpec,
40	) -> ScoreSet:
41	    """One float score per game under the spec's player and seed, and `record` games."""
42	    result = evaluate(make_env, agents, spec)
43	    assert isinstance(result, ScoreSet)
44	    assert result.player == spec.player
45	    assert result.train_seed == spec.train_seed
46	    assert result.scores.shape == (spec.n_games,)
47	    assert np.issubdtype(result.scores.dtype, np.floating)
48	    assert len(result.games) == spec.record
49	    assert all(isinstance(game, GameRecording) for game in result.games)
50	    return result
51

5	from rllab.core import SOLO

14	    scores = codec.build(ScoreSet, raw["scores"])

1	"""runs.RunWriter: an in-memory double, and the check any run writer must pass.
2	
3	`check_writer(writer, run, agent)` takes the provider, so the writer `runs.create_run`
4	returns runs the same check as the double.
5	"""
6	
7	from collections.abc import Sequence
8	from dataclasses import dataclass
9	from pathlib import Path
10	from typing import Literal
11	
12	import numpy as np
13	
14	from rllab.core import SOLO, Agent, RunSettings
15	from rllab.runs import CurvePoint, GameRecording, RunRecord, RunWriter
16	
17	
18	@dataclass(frozen=True)
19	class WriterRun:
20	    """What one recorded run handed its writer: settings, curve points and games."""
21	
22	    settings: RunSettings
23	    points: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]
24	    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]
25	
26	
27	class MemoryRunWriter:
28	    """Keeps every call in memory; agents save their checkpoints and generations under `path`."""
29	
30	    def __init__(self, path: Path, settings: RunSettings) -> None:
31	        self.path = path
32	        self.settings = settings
33	        self.points: list[CurvePoint] = []
34	        self.checkpoints: dict[str, Path] = {}
35	        self.generations: dict[str, list[Path]] = {}
36	        self.games: tuple[GameRecording, ...] = ()
37	        self.status = "running"
38	
39	    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None:
40	        self.points.append(p)
41	
42	    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = SOLO) -> None:
43	        path = self.path / f"checkpoint-{team}"
44	        agent.save(path)
45	        self.checkpoints[team] = path
46	
47	    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path:
48	        path = self.path / "generations" / team / str(index)
49	        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
50	        agent.save(path)
51	        self.generations.setdefault(team, []).append(path)
52	        return path
53	
54	    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None:
55	        self.games = tuple(games)
56	
57	    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord:
58	        self.status = status
59	        return RunRecord(
60	            path=self.path,
61	            settings=self.settings,
62	            curve=tuple(self.points),
63	            status=status,
64	            checkpoints=dict(self.checkpoints),
65	            generations={team: tuple(paths) for team, paths in self.generations.items()},
66	            games=self.games,
67	        )
68	
69	
70	def check_writer(writer: RunWriter | MemoryRunWriter, run: WriterRun, agent: Agent) -> RunRecord:
71	    """A run written point by point, with a checkpoint and games, finishes as one RunRecord."""
72	    for point in run.points:
73	        writer.add_point(point)
74	    writer.save_checkpoint(agent)
75	    writer.save_games(run.games)
76	    record = writer.finish("complete")
77	    assert isinstance(record, RunRecord)
78	    assert record.status == "complete"
79	    assert record.settings == run.settings
80	    assert record.curve == run.points
81	    assert set(record.checkpoints) == {SOLO}
82	    assert len(record.games) == len(run.games)
83	    for got, sent in zip(record.games, run.games, strict=True):
84	        np.testing.assert_array_equal(got.frames, sent.frames)
85	    return record
86

38-## Components
39-| Component | Responsibility | Covers | Depends on |
40-|---|---|---|---|
--
121:### AD-06: One folder per run; TOML settings in, plain files out
122-**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** `runs`
123-- **Context:** RUN-01 needs a run fully described by its settings. RUN-02 needs settings, seed, curve, checkpoint and games saved. RUN-05 needs reloading. The interrupted-run edge case needs partial records kept.
124-- **Decision:** Settings are read from TOML into a frozen `core.RunSettings`. Each run writes `runs/<name>-s<seed>-<timestamp>/` holding `settings.json` (the resolved settings), `curve.csv`, `checkpoint.pt` or `checkpoint.npz`, `games.npz` and `status` (`running`, `complete` or `incomplete`). The curve is appended as the run goes. An interrupt marks the run `incomplete`.
125-- **Alternatives:** An experiment tracker such as MLflow or W&B — a server or account for a one-person lab. One SQLite file — harder to inspect by eye and to delete one run from.
126-- **Consequences:** A run is inspectable with any text editor and deleted by removing its folder. Nothing indexes runs, so reports name their run folders explicitly.
127-

20	    """A `run_claims` returning `claims` in turn, one per spec."""
21	
22	    def run_claims(
23	        scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]],
24	        specs: Sequence[ClaimSpec],
25	        *,
26	        alpha: float = 0.05,
27	        charts: Sequence[Chart] = (),

1	"""viz.Replay: a windowless double stepping through a recording, and the provider check.
2	
3	`check_replay(replay, game)` takes the provider, so the real Replay (on a headless backend)
4	runs the same check as the double.
5	"""
6	
7	from rllab.runs import GameRecording
8	
9	
10	class ReplayDouble:
11	    """Frame indices only: `advance` moves on and stops at the last frame; `show` plays out."""
12	
13	    def __init__(self, game: GameRecording, *, fps: float = 8.0) -> None:
14	        self.game = game
15	        self.fps = fps
16	        self.index = 0
17	
18	    def advance(self) -> int:
19	        self.index = min(self.index + 1, len(self.game.frames) - 1)
20	        return self.index
21	
22	    def show(self) -> None:
23	        while self.index < len(self.game.frames) - 1:
24	            self.advance()
25	
26	
27	def check_replay(replay: ReplayDouble, game: GameRecording) -> None:
28	    """`advance` returns each next frame index, then stays at the last."""
29	    last = len(game.frames) - 1
30	    assert [replay.advance() for _ in range(last + 3)] == [*range(1, last + 1), last, last, last]
31

3	The fake raises `ValueError` for settings the contract refuses, and otherwise echoes the
4	sample with the given settings and a folder under `root`. `check_train_reference` takes the

7	
8	import itertools
9	from collections.abc import Callable
10	from dataclasses import replace
11	from pathlib import Path
12	
13	import pytest
14	
15	from rllab.core import SOLO, RunSettings
16	from rllab.runs import RunRecord
17	
18	
19	def supported(settings: RunSettings) -> bool:
20	    """Snake compact, the one-fish full-view tank, or the predator against scripted prey."""
21	    return settings.agent == "reference" and (
22	        (settings.env == "snake" and settings.view == "compact")
23	        or (settings.env == "tank" and settings.n_agents == 1 and settings.view == "full")
24	        or (
25	            settings.env == "predprey"
26	            and dict(settings.opponents) == {"predator": "learn", "prey": "scripted"}
27	        )
28	    )
29	
30	
31	def make_train_reference(record: RunRecord) -> Callable[..., RunRecord]:
32	    """A `train_reference` returning `record` for supported settings."""
33	
34	    def train_reference(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord:
35	        if not supported(settings):
36	            raise ValueError(f"the reference cannot train {settings.env}/{settings.view}")
37	        return replace(record, path=root / f"{settings.name}-s{settings.seed}", settings=settings)
38	
39	    return train_reference
40	
41	
42	def check_train_reference(
43	    train_reference: Callable[..., RunRecord],
44	    settings: RunSettings,
45	    refused: RunSettings,
46	    root: Path,
47	) -> RunRecord:
48	    """Snake reference settings finish complete with a curve from 0; `refused` raises."""
49	    record = train_reference(settings, root=root)
50	    assert isinstance(record, RunRecord)
51	    assert record.status == "complete"
52	    assert record.settings == settings
53	    assert record.path.parent == root
54	    steps = [point.step for point in record.curve]
55	    assert steps[0] == 0
56	    assert all(a < b for a, b in itertools.pairwise(steps))
57	    assert set(record.checkpoints) == {SOLO}
58	    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"\S"):  # any explanation
59	        train_reference(refused, root=root)
60	    return record
61
